Experiments on four verifier benchmarks demonstrate that AutoVerifier outperforms state-of-the-art verifiers by a large margin.

</details>


### [135] [Unmatched Does Not Mean False: Incomplete Reference Sets Can Reverse Calibration Rankings in Open-Ended Theory-of-Mind Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25654)
*Zhexi Feng,Wuxi Chen,Bingrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示开放域心智理论（ToM）追踪器评估中的一个严重缺陷：当模型输出在有限参考集合中不存在时，常用的"有限参考+匹配器"流程会错误地将这些输出标记为假，产生代理标签，从而系统性地扭曲评估结果。论文证明这种参考重编码可将固定输出上的加权流行率从0.783降至0.295，并反转严格适当的Brier风险排序；在ICE特定场景及NQ-open DPR-BERT管道中均观察到校准误差的反转。作者提出精确分解公式、闭式判据以及TriSource-Restore方法（将参考标签与冻结自动评判锚定到概率抽样的人类试点）来修复校准并抵御这些代理标签扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式心智理论追踪器会生成在有限参考集合中不存在但实际有效的信念，而现有评估管道将未匹配输出一律标记为假，产生代理标签。这种代理标签可能不仅无法反映真实性能，甚至可能反转模型选择的排序，导致对ToM系统能力的系统性误判。

Method: 论文采用实证与理论相结合的方法：(1) 使用259条固定信念和配对分数，通过参考重编码展示Brier风险反转；(2) 在301题的NQ-open DPR-BERT管道上检验ICE特定反转；(3) 在OpenToM叙事上审计未匹配信念的字面真实性（90-96%为真）；(4) 提出精确分解将扭曲归因于被省略的真实信念；(5) 设计闭式判据对12个发布系统的比较进行分类；(6) 提出TriSource-Restore方法，将全帧参考标签和冻结自动评判锚定到概率抽样的人类试点。

Result: 参考重编码使加权流行率从0.783降至0.295，使冻结源先验规则在参考标签下领先原生置信度0.227，但在盲审下落后0.152（六个场景全部复现）；仅参考的Platt重校准进一步反转。ICE特定反转在NQ管道中确认：平均置信度基线在精确匹配下校准误差改善0.045，但在人工正确性下恶化0.074，两个区间均排除零。冻结审计回溯重放显示50次注释尝试以至少0.996概率恢复排序方向。TriSource-Restore在维持名义覆盖率的同时收窄区间并修复置信度。

Conclusion: 有限参考加匹配器评估管线会产生系统性扭曲的代理标签，足以反转严格适当的评分规则下的模型排序。这是ToM评估中此前未被充分认识的方法论缺陷。TriSource-Restore通过锚定人类试点提供了可行的修复方案，但需要部署时进行基础率门槛检查。

Abstract: Open-ended Theory-of-Mind (ToM) trackers emit valid beliefs absent from finite references. A finite-reference-plus-matcher pipeline marks unmatched outputs false, creating proxy labels that can reverse proper-score model selection on fixed outputs. Holding 259 beliefs and paired scores fixed, reference recoding lowers weighted prevalence from 0.783 to 0.295 and reverses strictly proper Brier risk: a frozen source-prior rule leads native confidence by 0.227 under reference labels and trails by 0.152 under blinded adjudication, in all six authored scenarios. A reference-only Platt recalibrator reverses further. An ICE-specific reversal appears in a released 301-question NQ-open DPR-BERT pipeline: its average-confidence baseline improves instance-level calibration error by 0.045 under exact match but worsens it by 0.074 under human correctness, with both intervals excluding zero. On independently authored OpenToM narratives, 90-96% of audited unmatched beliefs are literally true and the paired direction again reverses. An exact decomposition attributes the distortion to omitted truths, and a closed-form criterion correctly classifies comparisons from twelve released systems. Frozen-audit retrospective replay shows 50 attempted annotations recover ranking direction with probability at least 0.996. TriSource-Restore anchors full-frame reference labels and frozen automatic judgments to a probability-sampled human pilot, maintains at least nominal coverage, narrows intervals, and repairs confidence subject to a base-rate deployment gate.

</details>


### [136] [Reconstructing the Right Episode: Evaluating Interleaved Conversational Memory Beyond Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25655)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE-QA，一个针对扁平无分段长对话线程的约束驱动任务QA基准，并提出了TSIM（时间语义交错记忆重建）方法，通过将对话流分段为连贯情节并用分层多视图记忆栈索引，在三个LLM后端上均超越强基线5.6-17.6个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文和记忆基准往往暴露会话或主题边界，或仅探测直接的个人记忆问题，这低估了更具挑战性的助手记忆场景：在扁平混合主题的线程中，系统必须推断哪个早期情节能使后续任务决策有效。这种"情节完整性失败"（episode integrity failure）问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者构建了SCALE-QA数据集，包含10个领域、3,000道经审计的多选题（确定性四选一评分），并通过确定性运行时构建器生成，实验覆盖128k全量集和1M的400题分层诊断集。同时提出TSIM方法：将轮次流分割为连贯情节，通过分层多视图记忆栈进行索引，包含确定性情节级摘要和聚类路由视图。

Result: 实验表明SCALE-QA对强RAG基线和长上下文LLM均构成挑战；在三个开源和专有LLM后端上，TSIM在所有后端设置中均取得最高准确率，超越最强对应基线5.6-17.6个准确率点。

Conclusion: SCALE-QA揭示了扁平无分段线程中情节完整性失效这一被低估的挑战，而TSIM通过时间与语义相结合的情节分段与多视图记忆索引，有效提升了多主题长对话中的任务决策能力。

Abstract: Conversations with chat assistants increasingly span many topics in a single long-running thread, challenging memory systems. Existing long-context and memory benchmarks often expose session or topic boundaries, or probe direct personal-memory questions. These settings understate a harder assistant-memory regime: a flat mixed-topic thread where the system must infer which earlier episode makes a later task decision valid. We introduce SCALE-QA, a constraint-grounded task QA benchmark for flat unsegmented threads targeting episode integrity failure. The dataset contains 3,000 audited questions across 10 domains, uses deterministic four-way multiple-choice grading, and includes a deterministic runtime builder; experiments use all 3,000 questions through 128k and a stratified 400-question diagnostic at 1M. SCALE-QA questions are ordinary task-oriented requests whose correct answer depends on causally related evidence introduced earlier in the conversation. We also propose Temporal-Semantic Interleaved Memory Reconstruction (TSIM), which segments the turn stream into coherent episodes and indexes them through a hierarchical multi-view memory stack with deterministic episode-level summary and cluster-routing views. Experiments show that SCALE-QA challenges strong RAG baselines and long-context LLMs alike; across three open-source and proprietary LLM backends, TSIM achieves the highest accuracy in every backend setting, gaining 5.6-17.6 accuracy points over the strongest corresponding baseline.

</details>


### [137] [Think-Probe-Respond: Improving Large Language Models as Judges of Research Idea Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25660)
*Tim Schopf,Tobias Schreieder,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在自动化新颖性判断中尽管生成的推理理由与人类专家高度相似，但最终判断结果却存在系统性偏差——倾向于将想法判断为"中等新颖"。作者提出Think-Probe-Respond (TPR)方法，通过在推理阶段从隐藏状态中探测潜在的新颖性判断来条件化最终响应，在强基线基础上将新颖性判断性能提升22.30%，并有效缓解"中等新颖"偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化新颖性判断可以加速科学发现进程，大语言模型被越来越多地用于此任务，但现有研究表明其判断能力存在一个被忽视的局限：尽管LLM生成的理由与人类专家相似，最终新颖性判断结果却往往与人类存在显著分歧，这种失校准源于模型系统性偏向于将想法判定为"中等新颖"。

Method: 提出Think-Probe-Respond (TPR)方法：在模型推理阶段从隐藏状态中探测潜在的（latent）新颖性判断，然后利用探测到的判断来条件化（condition）最终响应，从而纠正判断偏差。

Result: 在多个强基线上，TPR将新颖性判断性能提升22.30%，并成功缓解了普遍存在的"中等新颖"偏差。

Conclusion: 通过从隐藏状态中提取潜在判断并用于条件化最终输出，可以显著改善LLM在科学新颖性判断任务上的表现，同时解决系统性偏差问题，为自动化科学发现提供更可靠的支持。

Abstract: Automated novelty judgment can accelerate scientific discovery by enabling efficient evaluation, refinement, and comparison of research ideas. While large language models are increasingly adopted for this task, we investigate a previously overlooked limitation in their judgment capabilities: despite generating reasoning rationales that closely mirror those of human experts, their final novelty judgments often diverge substantially. We demonstrate that this miscalibration stems from a systematic bias towards judging ideas as "medium novel". To mitigate this, we propose Think-Probe-Respond (TPR), a lightweight approach that probes latent novelty judgments from hidden states during the reasoning phase and uses the probed judgments to condition the final response. Across strong baselines, TPR improves novelty judgment performance by 22.30% and successfully mitigates the prevalent "medium novelty" bias.

</details>


### [138] [Overview of SHROOM-Visions 2026: A Shared Task on Hallucination Detection in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25662)
*Raúl Vázquez,Aman Sinha,Chuyuan Li,Claudio Savelli,Eduardo Calò,Emilio Raimond,Stella Frank,Hengyu Luo,Flavio Giobergia,Vincent Segonne,Lorenzo Vaiani,Jörg Tiedemann,Timothee Mickus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SHROOM-Visions共享任务的第四轮迭代，该任务专注于大视觉语言模型中的幻觉检测，基于SHEEP数据集对图像条件下文本生成的细粒度幻觉跨度进行五类分类，覆盖四种语言，共27支队伍提交600+系统，最佳系统在各项指标上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续2024和2025年SHROOM任务的成功，旨在通过模型无关的检测任务解决大视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，并利用SHEEP数据集实现跨代模型生成内容的长期评估。

Method: 基于SHEEP数据集构建任务，要求参与者检测并分类图像条件下文本生成（如VQA、图像描述）中的细粒度幻觉跨度，采用涵盖四种语言（中文、英文、法文、意大利文）的五类幻觉分类体系，评估指标包括字符级相关、标签条件相关和交并比（IoU）。

Result: 任务吸引了全球NLP社区的广泛关注，27支队伍提交了600多个系统。最佳系统在四种语言上的平均得分为：字符级相关0.58、标签条件相关0.46、IoU 0.51，比基线高出30-40个百分点。

Conclusion: SHROOM-Visions共享任务成功推动了视觉语言模型幻觉检测的研究，最佳系统显著优于基线，但仍表明幻觉检测在跨语言和多类别分类场景下存在进一步提升的空间。

Abstract: In 2026, we held the fourth iteration of the SHROOM Shared Task series: SHROOM-Visions (\textbf{S}hared-task on \textbf{H}allucinations and \textbf{R}elated \textbf{O}bservable \textbf{O}vergeneration \textbf{M}istakes in \textbf{Vision} language model\textbf{s}), which is hosted at the UncertaiNLP Workshop co-located with EMNLP 2026. Following the success of the 2024 and 2025 tasks, this time we aim to tackle hallucinations through a model-agnostic detection task focused on large vision-language models. Building on the recently introduced SHEEP dataset, designed for long-term evaluation across model generations, the task invites participants to detect and classify fine-grained hallucination spans in image-conditioned text generation (VQA, image captioning, etc.). The evaluation uses a five-class taxonomy of hallucinations spanning four languages: Chinese, English, French, and Italian. The shared task generated strong interest in the NLP community worldwide, with 27 teams contributing 600+ system submissions. The best systems achieve average scores of 0.58 in character-level correlation, 0.46 in label-conditioned correlation, and 0.51 in intersection-over-union (IoU) across four languages, outperforming the baselines by 30-40 points.

</details>


### [139] [Learning New Facts with QLoRA: An Acquisition-Retention Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25677)
*Estelle Zheng,Sébastien Warichet,Emmanuel Helbert,Christophe Cerisara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数高效微调中适配器容量（秩）对事实获取与能力保留之间权衡的影响。在OpenStreetMap衍生的受控基准上，对Qwen3-4B进行全量微调与QLoRA（秩8-64）对比，发现秩越高事实获取能力越强但无关任务性能下降越明显，形成清晰的获取-保留前沿；全量微调则表现为保守基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调通常被认为由于仅更新少量参数而能保留预训练能力，但作者质疑这一假设强烈依赖于适配器容量。需要系统研究适配器秩对事实获取与能力灾难性遗忘之间权衡的影响。

Method: 在OpenStreetMap衍生的受控基准上，让Qwen3-4B学习匿名地理关联，同时记录无关能力保留情况。对比全量微调（FFT）与量化低秩适配（QLoRA）在秩8/16/32/64下的表现，结合分布、权重空间和谱诊断分析，并辅以一个数学适配实验作为对照。

Result: 秩诱导出清晰的获取-保留前沿：低秩QLoRA保留OOD性能但获取事实较少；高秩提升同事实释义泛化但增加无关基准性能损失。FFT作为保守基线，保留通用能力好但未达到最高事实获取区间。分布/权重/谱诊断与行为权衡一致，高秩QLoRA偏离预训练模型更远。数学适配实验显示更弱的前沿，表明该效应在新事实关联安装而非已有技能强化时最显著。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调保留预训练能力的前提并非必然成立，取决于适配器容量；高秩适配在获取新事实时会付出更大的能力遗忘代价。该效应在需要安装全新事实关联的场景中最突出，而强化预训练已支持的技能时影响较弱。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is often assumed to preserve pretrained capabilities because it updates only a small number of parameters. We show that this assumption depends strongly on adapter capacity. We study factual acquisition in a controlled OpenStreetMap-derived benchmark where Qwen3-4B must acquire anonymized geographic associations while retaining unrelated capabilities. Comparing full fine-tuning (FFT) with quantized low-rank adaptation (QLoRA) at ranks 8, 16, 32, and 64, we find that rank induces a clear acquisition--retention frontier. Low-rank QLoRA preserves out-of-domain (OOD) performance but acquires fewer facts, whereas higher ranks improve same-fact paraphrase generalization at an increasing cost in performance on unrelated benchmarks. FFT behaves as a conservative baseline: it retains general capabilities well, but does not reach the highest factual-acquisition regime. Distributional, weight-space, and spectral diagnostics mirror this behavioral trade-off, with higher-rank QLoRA moving farther from the pretrained model. A separate math adaptation experiment shows a weaker frontier, suggesting that the effect is most pronounced when adaptation must install new factual associations rather than reinforce skills already supported by pretraining. Code and data are available at https://github.com/zhngstl/new_facts_forgetting.

</details>


### [140] [When RAG Fails to Equalize: Geo-bias in Factual Question Answering over Public Companies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25717)
*Abhinav Havaldar,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成（RAG）能否均匀地弥补大语言模型（LLM）的知识缺失问题。通过构建包含约2000家全球上市公司的基准数据集，评估6个LLM在四种上下文条件下的表现，发现检索无法完全消除地理差异和知识缺口，RAG并非通用的纠错机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG被广泛认为可以缓解LLM的事实性错误，但现有研究尚未系统验证检索是否能够均匀地补偿模型缺失的知识。作者希望探究检索增强效果是否与模型的内部知识表征存在耦合关系。

Method: 构建了一个包含约2000家全球股权指数公司的受控事实问答基准，覆盖四个原子属性。对六个LLM在四种条件下进行评估：无上下文、完美上下文、误导上下文和干扰上下文，以系统比较检索对不同场景的影响。

Result: 发现：1）无上下文准确性存在显著的地理差异，反映参数化知识分布不均；2）完美上下文虽提升性能但无法消除这些差距，且增益与基线准确率相关，表明检索效果与内部表征耦合；3）误导上下文下模型频繁复制错误信息；4）更大规模的模型提升整体性能但无法消除这些结构性效应。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了RAG作为通用纠错机制的假设，强调了模型知识、上下文质量和实体表征之间的交互关系，提示RAG的有效性取决于模型的内部知识基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely assumed to mitigate factual errors in large language models (LLMs), but it remains unclear whether retrieval uniformly compensates for missing knowledge. We study this question in a controlled factual QA setting over public companies, constructing a benchmark of approximately 2,000 firms across global equity indices. We evaluate six LLMs on four atomic attributes under four conditions: no-context, perfect context, misleading context, and distraction context. We find strong geographic disparities in no-context accuracy, indicating uneven parametric knowledge. While perfect context improves performance, it does not eliminate these gaps: gains are correlated with baseline accuracy, suggesting retrieval effectiveness is coupled to internal representations. Under misleading context, models frequently copy incorrect information. Larger models improve overall performance but do not remove these structural effects. These results challenge the view of RAG as a universal corrective and highlight the interaction between model knowledge, context quality, and entity representation.

</details>


### [141] [Beam Search, Self-Consistency, and the Limits of Inference-Time Scaling for Grammar-Constrained Text-to-SQL in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25761)
*Ty Chermsirivatana,John MacCormick*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中"模型大小vs推理计算量"的权衡在受约束（严格语法）场景下的表现，发现其与无约束场景表现不同。在text-to-SQL任务上（Spider基准，Qwen2.5-Instruct系列0.5B至7B参数，4-bit精度），实验表明：束搜索和采样+投票均能提升准确率（尤其对小模型）；增大模型比增加推理计算更有效；在匹配推理预算下，束搜索优于采样+投票，这与无约束场景下的结论相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM使用中存在一个常见的权衡：减小模型规模同时增加推理时的计算量（如更宽的束搜索）。作者希望探索在这种权衡被严格文法约束的限制情况下，其行为是否与无约束场景有所不同，从而为实际部署中的模型选择和推理策略提供指导。

Method: 在Spider text-to-SQL基准上，使用Qwen2.5-Instruct模型族（0.5B至7B参数，均为4-bit精度），在严格文法约束下进行推理。采用两种增加推理计算的方法：（1）束搜索（改变束的数量）；（2）采样+投票（对多个约束输出采样并对其执行结果投票，改变采样数量）。在1034个示例的开发集上进行系统比较。

Result: (a) 束搜索和采样+投票都能提升准确率，尤其对较小模型效果更明显；(b) "模型大小vs推理计算"的权衡在此实验中不占优势——换用更大的模型通常比在相同模型上增加推理计算获得更高准确率；(c) 在匹配推理预算下，束搜索优于采样+投票，这与无约束权衡的发现形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 受约束（严格文法）的模型大小与推理计算权衡与无约束场景表现不同：在约束条件下，束搜索比采样+投票更有效，且增大模型规模相比增加推理计算是更优的投资方向。

Abstract: One common trade-off in the use of large language models involves reducing the size of the model while increasing the amount of computation at inference time, for example by using a wider beam search. In this paper, we examine the constrained case of this "model size vs. inference compute" trade-off, in which the model outputs are constrained by a strict grammar at inference time. Our results demonstrate that the constrained trade-off behaves differently from the unconstrained trade-off. We investigate the task of converting a prose query into an equivalent SQL query (text-to-SQL). Performance is evaluated on the Spider text-to-SQL benchmark, using the Qwen2.5-Instruct model family ranging in size from 0.5B to 7B parameters, all at 4-bit precision. We experiment with two approaches to varying inference compute: (i) beam search with a variable number of beams; and (ii) sample+vote, i.e., sampling several constrained outputs and then voting on their execution results, where the number of samples is varied. On the 1034-example development set, we find that: (a) both beam search and sample+vote improve accuracy, especially on smaller model sizes; (b) the "model size vs.\ inference compute" trade-off is not advantageous in this experiment, because moving to a larger model size typically results in higher accuracy than increasing inference compute on the same model size; (c) beam search outperforms sample+vote at a matched inference budget. This latter result is of particular interest since it contrasts with the findings of the unconstrained trade-off.

</details>


### [142] [MoganBert-TR: A Turkish Encoder Foundation Model Trained from Scratch with a CLM-to-MLM Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25768)
*Furkan Yilmaz,Habibe Aleyna Tasdemir,Muhammed Faruk Gozay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MoganBert-TR，一个149M参数的土耳其语编码器基础模型，采用两阶段CLM-to-MLM课程学习策略，在237.3B tokens上训练，并衍生出嵌入模型MoganBert-Embed。该模型在TrGLUE（78.41）、TabiBench（77.73）和MTEB土耳其语整体平均（68.30）上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的土耳其语编码器模型虽然采用了现代架构，但预训练目标仍固定在掩码语言建模（MLM）上，没有探索其他预训练策略。作者认为这种固定范式可能不是最优的，特别是在嵌入质量和检索任务上存在局限性。

Method: 1) 采用两阶段CLM-to-MLM课程：先因果语言建模，后掩码语言建模，转换点在WSD调度的稳定阶段。2) 长上下文扩展和学习率衰减分成两个分支：在1024上下文下运行最终衰减。3) MoganBert-Embed通过教师蒸馏和多信号对比微调得到。4) 附带50,048-token分词器。

Result: 课程学习在同等步数预算下比纯MLM在土耳其MS MARCO检索上提升2.7-3.7倍；1024上下文衰减在TrGLUE上提升0.49±0.26分（p=0.013）；MoganBert-TR在TrGLUE得78.41，TabiBench得77.73；MoganBert-Embed在MTEB(土耳其)整体平均68.30，以51倍更小的骨干达到教师模型99.5%的性能。

Conclusion: 两阶段CLM-to-MLM课程训练策略显著改善了嵌入几何（退化方向方差从28.1%降至11.9%），并在检索和下游任务上超越纯MLM。模型权重、分词器、嵌入模型和评估代码均已开源。

Abstract: Turkish encoder models have adopted modern architectures while leaving the pretraining objective fixed at masked language modelling. This paper introduces MoganBert-TR, a 149M-parameter Turkish encoder foundation model trained from scratch on a language-specifically filtered corpus, together with an embedding model derived from it (MoganBert-Embed).
  MoganBert-TR is trained over 237.3B tokens with a two-stage CLM-to-MLM curriculum: causal language modelling first, masked language modelling for the remainder, with the transition made inside the stable phase of a WSD schedule. In a controlled ablation under an equal step budget, this design outperforms pure MLM by 2.7-3.7x on Turkish MS MARCO retrieval; the measured mechanism is embedding geometry, where a single direction absorbs 28.1% of the variance under pure MLM against 11.9% under the curriculum. Long-context extension and learning-rate decay are then split into two branches after a shared prefix: running the final portion of decay at 1024 context improves the TrGLUE average by 0.49 +/- 0.26 points across five paired seeds (p = 0.013) and beats a model-soup alternative by 0.75 points at ~4.3% additional cost.
  MoganBert-TR attains 78.41 on TrGLUE, the best among the Turkish ModernBERT models compared, and 77.73 on TabiBench, where it leads two of the eight categories with the largest margin on code retrieval (+3.62 points over TabiBERT). MoganBert-Embed, produced through teacher distillation and multi-signal contrastive fine-tuning, ranks first among student models on the MTEB(Turkish) overall average with 68.30 and reaches 99.5% of its 7.57B-parameter teacher's score with a 51x smaller backbone. The accompanying 50,048-token tokenizer outperforms all compared Turkish tokenizers on compression and fertility across two independent test sets. Weights, tokenizer, embedding model and evaluation code: https://huggingface.co/moganai

</details>


### [143] [Localize-Then-Decide Guarantees for LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25824)
*Xinyu Li,Yi Zhou,Guanqun Cao,Zeyu Fu,Tianjin Huang,Gaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Localize-Then-Decide框架，通过两阶段方法（先共形预测定位包含人类偏好答案的候选短列表，再基于校准置信度从短列表中选最终答案或弃权），解决大规模语言模型作为评估器时置信度与人类判断风险单调性失效的问题，实现高概率的一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估器时，现有的置信度阈值方法依赖"更高置信度意味着更低与人类不一致风险"的假设，但当候选响应数量增多时，概率质量分散到多个选项会扭曲置信度估计，导致该假设失效，无法提供可靠的一致性与人类判断的保证。

Method: 提出Localize-Then-Decide两阶段框架：(1) Localize阶段，利用共形预测（conformal prediction）定位一个较小的候选短列表，使其以高概率包含人类偏好的响应；(2) Decide阶段，在该短列表上采用校准后的基于置信度的规则，选择性输出单个答案或弃权。该设计恢复置信度与不一致风险之间的单调关系。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同评判LLM上，针对多种候选规模的实验中，所提框架相比单阶段基线方法，在保证成功率（guarantee success rates）和覆盖率（coverage）方面均持续达到更高水平。

Conclusion: Localize-Then-Decide框架通过先缩小候选范围再决定输出的两阶段策略，有效修复了候选数量增加时置信度估计被扭曲的问题，从而为LLM评估器提供更可靠且覆盖率更高的与人类判断一致性的概率保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as evaluators to assess output quality and preference alignment, yet providing reliable guarantees of agreement with human judgments remains challenging. Recent work introduces confidence-thresholding methods that provide such guarantees for pairwise comparisons, relying on the assumption that higher estimated confidence implies lower disagreement risk with humans. However, this assumption can break down when the number of candidate responses increases, since distributing probability mass across many alternatives can distort confidence estimates. To address this issue, we propose a Localize-Then-Decide framework. First, conformal prediction localizes a small shortlist that contains the human-preferred response with high probability. Then, a calibrated confidence-based rule selectively chooses a single response from this shortlist or abstains. This design restores the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk and enables high-probability agreement guarantees. Experiments with multiple candidate sizes across several datasets and judge LLMs demonstrate that our framework consistently achieves higher guarantee success rates and substantially higher coverage than single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Unfolding Scientific Papers into Multi-Turn Generation Trajectories for Continued Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25826)
*Qiankai Xu,Qiguang Chen,Zixin Su,Wenhao Huang,Yue Gao,Jiaheng Liu,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将学术论文"展开"为多轮生成轨迹的流水线，通过教师模型重建整篇论文的写作过程（写作请求、全局规划、各章节写作前思考），论文正文保持原样，由此构建约两倍于原文规模的预训练语料，并延伸出SFT指令数据与学术写作评测基准PAW-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的合成数据工作虽然能重建文本背后的思考过程，但仅适用于短网页片段、只能恢复局部思维，无法覆盖整篇文档的结构。科学论文具有清晰且大致统一的写作结构，是将其范式从片段级提升到文档级的天然载体。

Method: 设计一个流水线，将每篇论文展开为多轮生成轨迹：教师模型依次重建写作请求、全局计划和每个章节的写作前推敲，论文正文与摘要逐字保留。该流水线应用于质量过滤后的arXiv论文，得到用于继续预训练（CPT）的语料（约原文两倍大小）；同一逆向构建过程扩展到指令数据和评测——以真实论文文本为答案构成SFT数据集，基于留出论文构造PAW-Bench学术写作基准（自带评分标准与检查清单）。

Result: 受控实验表明，先在该语料上CPT再在公开数据集上SFT，能在广泛写作基准上提升表现，同时保持通用推理能力并改善长文档阅读。写作增益在每模型均使用专用写作SFT数据微调后依然存在；将该SFT数据混入配方可进一步提学术写作水平。

Conclusion: 将"重建思考而非改写正文"的范式从短文本提升到整篇学术论文是可行的，由此生成的合成轨迹语料不仅可作为CPT数据提升写作与长文档阅读能力，还能同时构建SFT数据与自含评分标准的评测基准，形成了"数据-训练-评测"闭环。

Abstract: A recent line of synthetic-data work reconstructs the thinking behind existing text rather than rewriting the text itself, but it operates on short web passages, recovers only local thoughts, and leaves the structure of whole documents untouched. Scientific papers are written to a clear and largely uniform structure and make a natural substrate for lifting this paradigm to the document level. We present a pipeline that unfolds each paper into a multi-turn generation trajectory in which a teacher model reconstructs the writing process of the whole paper: a writing request, a global plan, and pre-writing deliberation for each section. All section texts and the abstract are kept verbatim from the source paper. We apply the pipeline to quality-filtered arXiv papers and obtain a corpus for continued pre-training (CPT) that is roughly twice the size of the source text. The same reverse construction extends to instruction data and evaluation. Treating real paper text as the answer yields an SFT dataset. Anchoring tasks in held-out papers yields PAW-Bench, an academic-writing benchmark whose tasks carry their own rubrics and checklists. In controlled experiments CPT on our corpus followed by supervised fine-tuning on public datasets improves writing benchmarks broadly while preserving general reasoning and improving long-document reading. The writing gain persists even when every model is fine-tuned on a dedicated writing SFT dataset. Mixing our SFT data into that recipe lifts academic writing further.

</details>


### [145] [Key Point Analysis Needs Structure Recovery: Task Definition, Dataset Diagnosis, and a Structure-Aware Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25854)
*Zhiqiang Shi,Oana Cocarascu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KPA本质上是结构化预测问题，指出现有基准在分组质量、冗余、覆盖率和映射上存在缺陷，并引入一个通过人工干预重标注构建的结构感知、分布敏感的基准，以支持真正的KPA研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KPA基准在分组质量、冗余性、覆盖率和论点-关键点映射方面存在局限，导致基于参考的评估出现天花板违规和选择失败，无法真实反映KPA系统的性能。

Method: 将KPA形式化为包含语义分组、代表性关键点生成、覆盖率和流行度估计的结构化预测问题；通过人类参与的迭代重标注构建结构感知、分布敏感的新基准，并进行人工与LLM双重评估。

Result: 新基准在分组一致性、关键点质量、覆盖率和流行度估计可靠性上均优于现有标注；同时发布了多项标注资源以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: 真正的KPA需要以结构化预测为依据重新设计数据与评估方法，作者提供了新基准、资源和研究议程以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Key Point Analysis (KPA) aims to identify a concise set of key points that summarize a collection of arguments together with their prevalence. We argue that KPA is fundamentally a structured prediction problem that requires recovering semantic groupings, generating representative key points, ensuring coverage, and estimating prevalence. Under this formulation, we show that existing KPA benchmarks suffer from limitations in grouping quality, redundancy, coverage, and argument-key point mappings, causing ceiling violation and selection failure in reference-based evaluation. To support future research on true KPA, we introduce a structure-aware, distribution-sensitive benchmark built via a human-in-the-loop re-annotation. Human and LLM evaluations consistently show that the resulting structures yield more coherent groupings, higher-quality key points, better coverage, and more reliable prevalence estimates than existing annotations. We further release several annotation resources to support research on KPA evaluation, argument-key point matching, explainable KPA, and LLM-as-a-judge methodologies, and outline a research agenda for true KPA.

</details>


### [146] [Anchoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge Systems: Prior Scores Compromise Evaluation Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25869)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Kemal Altwlkany,Andro Mercep,Tomislav Duricic,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（192,000次评估，185,271次成功）证明LLM-as-a-Judge范式存在锚定偏差：即使先前分数仅作为上下文元数据出现，也会系统性偏移评分结果。8个模型中7个显示显著的锚定效应，Cohen's d绝对值最高达0.71；锚定元数据在分类任务中阻断48%的错误修正并翻转10.18%的正确判断；链式思维提示和元数据忽略警告均无法消除该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge系统在生产管线中被广泛用于评分、内容过滤和迭代精炼的门控，每个判断通常被假定为独立于此前评估。作者旨在检验这一独立性假设是否成立，特别是先前评分作为上下文元数据时是否引入锚定偏差。

Method: 设计了三种提示条件：无元数据（基线）、修订框架（revision framing）、含修订/尝试次数/先前评分字段的锚定元数据。对20个固定文本进行192,000次评估，使用95%任务分层自助法（bootstrap）区间检验总锚定效应；计算Cohen's d标准化效应量；进行token级概率分布分析揭示阈值型响应模式；额外的分类任务实验使用人工标注的真实标签检验偏差对分类决策的影响；测试链式思维（CoT）和忽略元数据警告的缓解效果。

Result: 7/8模型中，锚定元数据对20个固定文本的总效应95%任务分层自助区间低于零，效应显著；Cohen's d绝对值达0.71。Token级分析显示类似阈值型响应：引入锚定元数据导致输出分数概率显著再分配，但锚定值在阈值以下范围内变化时额外变异很小。分类实验中，锚定元数据阻断48%的错误修正并翻转10.18%的正确判断。CoT和元数据忽略警告均无法减少总效应，但警告在行业实验中改善了配对准确率效应。

Conclusion: LLM评估的可靠性不能依赖无偏性假设，必须谨慎进行上下文工程。有效缓解措施需要针对目标模型和任务/领域进行验证，简单提示策略不足以消除锚定偏差。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly assess generated content, giving rise to the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. These systems now score outputs, filter content, and gate iterative refinement in production pipelines, where each judgment is often assumed to be independent of earlier evaluations. We test this assumption using three prompt conditions: no metadata, revision framing, and anchored metadata containing revision, attempt, and prior-score fields. We show that prior scores, even when included only as context metadata, anchor judgments and systematically shift ratings toward their values. Across 192,000 attempted evaluations (185,271 successful), seven out of the eight evaluated models have 95% task-stratified bootstrap intervals below zero for the total anchored-metadata effect on 20 fixed texts. Cohen's $d$, a standardized measure of the difference between score distributions, reaches an absolute value of 0.71. Token-level analysis of selected model-task probes suggests a threshold-like response pattern: introducing anchored metadata produces a marked redistribution of output-score probabilities, while changing the anchor value within the tested below-threshold range produces comparatively little additional variation. On categorical industry data with human-labeled ground truth, anchored metadata blocks 48% of error corrections and flips 10.18% of correct judgments toward an assigned wrong label, demonstrating the bias extends beyond numerical scoring to categorical decisions. Neither Chain-of-Thought nor a metadata-disregard warning reduces the total effect, although the warning improves the paired accuracy effect relative to baseline in the industry experiment. Reliable LLM evaluation demands careful context engineering rather than an assumption of impartiality. Effective mitigation must be validated for the intended model and task or domain.

</details>


### [147] [Loss-Based Active Learning for Neural Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25881)
*Michail Ioannou,Tatiana Passali,George Michalopoulos,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LOBSTER框架，一种针对抽象式文本摘要的主动学习方法，通过选择与模型当前高损失训练样本语义相似的无标注实例进行标注，以纠正模型特定弱点，在三个基准数据集上以高达665倍的查询速度提升达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象式摘要模型的微调需要高质量标注数据，但人工标注成本高昂且耗时。主动学习虽能减少标注需求，但应用于摘要任务时缺乏探索，现有方法常存在不稳定性且计算开销大。

Method: 提出LOBSTER框架，核心思想是优先选择与模型当前高损失训练样本在语义上相似的无标注实例进行标注，从而使模型能够显式地纠正其特定弱点。该方法基于损失驱动（loss-based）的主动学习策略，通过语义相似性匹配实现高效的样本选择。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和两个摘要骨干模型上的实验表明，LOBSTER一致地匹配或超越了当前最先进的方法，同时查询选择速度提升高达665倍。

Conclusion: LOBSTER通过基于损失和语义相似性的主动学习策略，有效解决了抽象式摘要任务中主动学习的不稳定性和计算瓶颈问题，在保持性能的同时大幅提升了查询效率。

Abstract: Fine-tuning abstractive summarization models requires high-quality annotated data. However, obtaining such corpora is expensive and time-consuming, as it requires human annotators to read and comprehend long documents to create accurate summaries. Active learning mitigates this issue by selecting only the most informative instances for annotation, allowing models to achieve competitive results with significantly fewer labels. However, the application of active learning to summarization remains under-explored, and existing studies often suffer from instability and significant computational bottlenecks. To overcome these challenges, we propose LOBSTER (LOss-BaSed acTivE leaRning), a novel active learning framework designed specifically for abstractive summarization. LOBSTER improves performance by prioritizing unlabeled instances semantically similar to the model's current high-loss training examples, enabling the model to explicitly correct its specific weaknesses. Our empirical evaluation across three benchmark datasets and two summarization backbone models demonstrates that LOBSTER consistently matches or outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches while achieving a query selection speedup of up to 665x.

</details>


### [148] [From Passive Response to Proactive Correction: Enhancing LLM Robustness Against Input Fact Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25894)
*Ping Wang,Xiangguo Sun,Bingbing Xu,Guocong Li,Xiaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DEDUCE三阶段框架，将大语言模型从被动应答者转变为主动纠错者，以应对用户输入中含误导性事实前提导致的幻觉问题，并在TruthfulQA、FalseQA和新提出的MisFactQA基准上验证了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对包含误导性前提的用户输入时，经常会自信地给出与事实不符的回答。现有幻觉缓解方法通常假设用户输入是可靠的，忽略了输入中事实错误对模型推理的主动误导作用。这一漏洞缺乏针对性研究。

Method: DEDUCE框架包含三个阶段：(1) 通过细粒度事实抽取与验证来检测输入中的错误；(2) 通过多视角深思来制定纠正策略；(3) 纠正用户误解的同时提供可靠的回答。此外还构建了MisFactQA数据集（含不同程度的事实错误）并提出了新的模型鲁棒性评估指标。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FalseQA和MisFactQA基准上的实验表明，DEDUCE显著提升了模型在误导性输入下的准确性和错误纠正能力。在Qwen、LLaMA和Gemma等多个模型家族上均取得了一致的性能提升，验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: DEDUCE通过主动检测并纠正用户输入中的事实扰动，有效解决了LLM在误导性前提下的幻觉问题，具有较强的通用性和跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce confident yet factually incorrect responses when user inputs contain misleading premises, a phenomenon we attribute to fact perturbations in the input. Existing approaches to hallucination mitigation typically assume reliable user inputs, overlooking how such factual errors can actively mislead model reasoning. To address this vulnerability, we propose DEDUCE, a three-stage framework that transforms LLMs from passive responders into proactive error correctors. DEDUCE operates in three stages: (1) detect errors through fine-grained fact extraction and verification; (2) devise correction strategies via multi perspective deliberation; and (3) correct misconceptions while delivering reliable answers. We also present MisFactQA, a dataset containing factual errors of varying degrees, and propose new metrics for evaluating model robustness. Experiments on TruthfulQA, FalseQA, and our MisFactQA benchmark demonstrate that DEDUCE significantly improves both accuracy and error correction capability. Consistent gains across Qwen, LLaMA, and Gemma families confirm its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [149] [One Form to Transfer Them All: Pretraining Multilingual Language Models Beyond Native Orthography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25904)
*Muge Zhang,Aaron Jencks,Krishna Badikela,Yulia Tsvetkov,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在自回归多语言预训练中系统比较了三种输入表示（正字法文本、IPA和罗马化），在三个规模（467M、709M、1.03B）和八个语言的四个类型学配对组合上进行了受控实验。结果显示罗马化预训练在跨语言迁移上最强，且随规模增加优势扩大；IPA在多数情况下优于文本但不及罗马化；对已在基础模型覆盖的语言进行罗马化微调反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言语言模型通过共享子词词汇实现跨语言知识迁移，但该机制在相关语言使用不同书写系统时会失效。先前研究通过脚本等化（罗马化或IPA转写）解决此问题，但缺乏系统的直接比较，且主要集中在编码器模型和对现有预训练模型的适配，缺少对自回归多语言预训练中输入表示的系统性研究。

Method: 在受控条件下，对三种输入表示（正字法文本、IPA、罗马化）进行自回归多语言预训练的系统比较。实验覆盖三个参数量规模（467M、709M、1.03B）和八个语言（构成四个类型学配对），并在多种下游任务上评估对已见和未见语言的跨语言迁移性能。

Result: 罗马化预训练在所有下游任务上产生最强的跨语言迁移效果，且相对于正字法文本的优势随模型规模增大而扩大；IPA在大多数设置下优于正字法文本但落后于罗马化；在文本预训练模型上进行罗马化微调会损害已在基础模型覆盖的语言的性能，仅在模型缺乏脚本覆盖时产生边际帮助。

Conclusion: 对于涵盖类型学上多样脚本的多语言模型，若要获得最大收益，罗马化应作为预训练阶段的核心设计选择，而非事后修补手段。

Abstract: Multilingual language models transfer knowledge across languages through shared subword vocabulary, a mechanism that breaks down when related languages use different writing systems. Prior work addresses this via script equalization (romanization or IPA transcription), but direct comparisons are rare; the focus has been on encoder-only models, with most work adapting existing pretrained models. We systematically compare different input representations in autoregressive multilingual pretraining, comparing orthographic text, IPA, and romanization in a controlled setup across three scales (467M, 709M, and 1.03B) on eight languages in four typologically motivated pairs. Across a wide range of downstream tasks on seen and unseen languages, romanized pretraining yields the strongest cross-lingual transfer, and the advantage over text widens with scale. IPA improves over text in most settings but trails romanization. Surprisingly, finetuning a text-pretrained model on romanized data hurts performance on languages already covered by the base model, only marginally helping when the model lacks script coverage. Our results indicate that for multilingual models spanning typologically diverse scripts, to obtain maximum benefits, romanization should be treated as a core design choice applied at pretraining rather than a post hoc fix.

</details>


### [150] [SAMpLE: A SystemC-AMS Machine LEarning-based Framework for Virtual Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25910)
*Andrei Mihai Albu,Sara Vinco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAMpLE是一个开源的基于SystemC-AMS的框架，将机器学习模型作为一等Timed Dataflow组件集成到虚拟平台仿真中，通过标准化即插即用接口和ONNX标准格式，支持在线训练和离线执行两种后端，实现ML模型的复用、可比较性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统虚拟原型中越来越多地使用机器学习来建模难以解析化描述的行为，但现有的ML模型集成通常采用临时解决方案，限制了模型的复用性、可比较性和可复现性。

Method: SAMpLE框架将ML模型作为一等Timed Dataflow(TDF)组件集成，提供两种执行后端：(1)原生C++后端用于轻量模型的在线训练；(2)离线后端执行外部开发的模型，无需重新用C++实现或手动集成。使用ONNX作为标准模型交换格式，支持在相同的测试平台、数据集和仿真工作流中评估不同的ML方案。

Result: 框架实现了标准的即插即用接口，支持通过ONNX格式集成外部训练的ML模型，在统一的测试环境和仿真工作流中评估不同ML解决方案，模块化设计允许未来扩展新模型而无需修改SystemC-AMS结构。

Conclusion: SAMpLE通过标准化接口和模块化设计，为ML模型与虚拟平台仿真的集成提供了统一、可复现的环境，解决了现有临时方案的复用性和可比较性问题，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) is increasingly used in virtual prototypes of embedded systems to model behaviors that are difficult to capture analytically. However, integrating ML models into virtual platform simulation is still typically done through ad hoc solutions, which limits reuse, comparability, and reproducibility. This paper presents \textbf{\textit{SAMpLE}}, an open-source SystemC-AMS-based framework that integrates ML models as first-class Timed Dataflow (TDF) components through a standardized plug-and-play interface. SAMpLE provides two execution backends: a native C++ backend for online training of lightweight models, and an offline backend for executing externally developed models without requiring re-implementation in C++ or manual integration steps. The framework uses ONNX as a standard model exchange format to enable integration of externally trained ML models into SystemC-AMS simulations, and allows the evaluation of different ML-based solutions within the same testbench, dataset, and simulation workflow. The modular design and unified and reproducible environment will allow future extensions of SAMpLE to new models, without modifying the SystemC-AMS structure.

</details>


### [151] [Query-Side Attacks on GNN-Based KGQA: Tracing Failures from Entity Linking to Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25922)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个阶段隔离协议，通过两种保真对抗扰动（CR和RS）来分别测试KGQA流水线的不同阶段。结果发现子图构建而非GNN推理是端到端性能崩溃的主要来源，CR扰动下子图构建占总崩溃的99%以上，即使在74%的检索子图中存在正确答案的情况下也会失败，揭示了"答案存在"与"答案可达"之间的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KGQA鲁棒性评估将所有阶段（实体链接、子图检索、GNN推理、答案生成）的失败混合为单一端到端指标，无法定位系统脆弱的真正来源，也无法确定应该针对哪个阶段进行修复。作者希望回答"当输入问题受到对抗扰动时，是哪一阶段失败了，以及为什么失败"这一关键问题。

Method: 提出阶段隔离协议，设计两种保真对抗扰动：（1）组合重构（CR）——重组问题的组合结构，保留实体种子，针对子图检索阶段；（2）关系同义词替换（RS）——替换关系表达为同义词，针对GNN推理阶段。两种扰动均通过知识图谱验证保证答案保守性，在ComplexWebQuestions和WebQSP数据集上进行评估，并对检索到的子图进行金答案存在性检查以区分答案存在与答案可达。

Result: 实验结果与主流假设相反：当子图完整时GNN推理阶段保持接近基线的准确率，而子图构建阶段在CR扰动下贡献了端到端性能崩溃的99%以上。更关键的是，即使在74%的检索子图中金答案实际存在，子图构建仍导致失败，说明问题不在于推理无法找到答案，而在于子图检索阶段破坏了答案的可达性。实验数据和评估基础设施已开源。

Conclusion: KGQA系统的鲁棒性瓶颈在于子图构建阶段而非GNN推理阶段。修复应聚焦于子图检索/构建模块，而非推理模型。端到端指标无法暴露这一区别，需要阶段级别的隔离评估来定位具体的修复方向。

Abstract: GNN-based Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) pipelines process queries through four discrete stages: entity linking, subgraph retrieval, GNN reasoning, and answer generation. Standard robustness evaluations conflate stage-level failures into a single end-to-end metric, obscuring both the source of brittleness and the appropriate mitigation target. We ask which stage fails, and why, when the pipeline is subjected to adversarial perturbations on the input question. We introduce a stage-isolation protocol with two answer-preserving adversarial perturbations verified against the knowledge graph: Compositional Restructuring (CR) and Relation Synonym Swap (RS) target distinct stages while leaving entity seeds intact. Evaluated across ComplexWebQuestions and WebQSP, the results run counter to prevailing assumptions: the GNN reasoning stage retains near-baseline accuracy when the subgraph is intact, while subgraph construction accounts for over 99\% of the end-to-end collapse under CR, occurring even when the gold answer is present in 74\% of retrieved subgraphs. This exposes a fundamental distinction between answer presence and answer reachability that end-to-end metrics cannot detect, and places the mitigation target firmly at the subgraph construction stage rather than the reasoning model. Perturbed datasets and evaluation infrastructure are released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/atkgrag-E85C .

</details>


### [152] [Unveiling Spectral Mechanisms in Training-Free LLM Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25944)
*Haitong Luo,Xuying Meng,Weiyao Zhang,Wenji Zou,Shengfeng Lou,Xuefeng Jiang,Chungang Lin,Yujun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实证两个角度系统分析了基于频谱分析的LLM文本检测方法，将其与置信度指标结合，揭示了频域检测的适用边界和信号机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的快速发展，人类写作与机器生成文本的区分越来越困难。虽然无需训练的检测方法具有可扩展性，但常见的基于置信度的指标主要测量平均token概率，忽略了人类写作中特有的信号波动（即"生成活力"）。频谱分析有望捕捉这种活力，但其工作机制和实际适用边界尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从理论层面将频谱能量与代理对数概率轨迹的方差联系起来，解释人类更广泛的token选择如何产生频域指标所利用的波动；从实证层面分析文本长度和采样范围对频谱信号强度的影响，并探讨在短文本、碎片化、混合及编辑等场景下需要结合置信度和波动视角的多维检测方案。

Result: 频谱证据在长篇幅、连续且受约束的生成文本中最为清晰；在短文本、碎片化、混合及编辑等设置下，需要置信度和波动视角的互补组合来提升检测效果。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了频域检测何时有效，并为未来多维检测器的设计提供了指导方向。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) makes it increasingly difficult to distinguish human writing from machine-generated text. Training-free detection offers a scalable solution, yet common confidence-based metrics mainly measure average token probabilities and often miss the signal fluctuations that characterize human writing, which we call "generative vitality". Spectral analysis offers a way to capture this vitality, but its mechanism and practical boundaries remain underexplored. In this paper, we analyze spectral detection from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. We connect spectral energy to variance in proxy log-probability trajectories and explain how broader human token choices create the fluctuations used by frequency-domain indicators. We further show that the strength of this signal depends on text length and sampling range: spectral evidence is clearest for long, continuous, constrained generation, while short, fragmented, mixed, and edited settings require complementary confidence and fluctuation views. These findings clarify when frequency-domain detection works and provide guidance for future multi-dimensional detector design.

</details>


### [153] [Lost but not erased: Finding traces of a forgotten language in neural speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25976)
*Peter Plantinga,Charlotte Moore,Peter W. Donhauser,Krista Byers-Heinlein,Denise Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用自动语音识别模型模拟国际领养者的语言经历，发现"关键期效应"并非源于生物成熟时间窗口，而是早期接触的第一语言在最低（前音素）层留下功能性痕迹，使模型重新学习该语言时比新手快14%。这表明关键期效应反映的是基础表征的固着，而非成熟性可塑性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际领养者即使无法再说或理解出生语言，仍保留其音韵痕迹，这一现象传统上被归因于生物计时性的关键期。作者质疑这一解释，想探究它是否可能只是普通学习动力学的自然结果，从而在没有成熟性混杂因素的条件下检验经验在关键期中的核心作用。

Method: 使用自动语音识别（ASR）模型模拟国际领养者经历：先训练一种语言，再突然切换到第二种语言。通过分析第一语言痕迹在第二语言训练中的存留位置，并通过消融实验（更换最早层为非领养模型、比较从相关语言"领养"的模型）来检验痕迹的功能性及来源。

Result: 第一语言的痕迹在整个第二语言训练过程中持续存在，但主要集中在最低的前音素层。这些痕迹具有功能性：早期暴露的模型重新学习遗失的出生语言速度快14%；即使与早期从相关语言领养的模型相比，该优势依然成立；当最早层被替换为非领养模型时，该优势消失。

Conclusion: 关键期效应应被理解为基础表征的固着（entrenchment），而非成熟性可塑性丧失。经验在语言习得的关键期中扮演核心角色，为"领养悖论"提供了一个不依赖生物学时间窗口的解释框架。

Abstract: International adoptees retain phonological traces of a birth language they can no longer speak or comprehend, a persistence typically attributed to a biologically-timed critical period. We asked whether it could instead reflect the ordinary dynamics of learning, using automatic speech recognition models that simulate the international adoptee experience without maturational confounds. Models were trained on one language and then abruptly switched to a second. We found that traces of the first language persisted throughout second-language training, but mainly in the lowest, pre-phonemic layers. These traces were functional, as models with early exposure re-learned their lost first language 14% faster than naive models; this advantage held even against models adopted early from a related language and disappeared when the earliest layers were substituted from a non-adopted model. We argue that these critical-period effects reflect entrenchment of foundational representations rather than a maturational loss of plasticity, and that experience plays a central role in critical periods in language acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [When Personality Meets Quantization: A Layer-wise MBTI Analysis of Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25977)
*Yao Fu,Lijia Huang,Xiaomin Li,Runchao Li,Yu Yin,Kenneth A. Loparo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究量化LLM（包括4-bit的GPTQ/AWQ和2-bit的AQLM变体）在不同精度下的MBTI人格特性和人格涌现机制，提出不确定性放大层解码（UALD）方法，揭示人格并非静态属性，而是受量化、提示和推理解码影响的、逐层涌现的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格研究仅关注全精度模型且只评估最终输出，忽略了广泛部署的低内存占用量化模型的人格特性，同时缺乏对人格在模型内部层级的动态涌现过程的理解。

Method: 对多种开源LLM在多个精度（4-bit GPTQ/AWQ、2-bit AQLM变体）下进行系统性MBTI分析；引入选项级熵和置信度差距动态来观察人格跨层涌现；提出UALD（不确定性放大层解码）方法研究推理时解码引起的人格漂移。

Result: （1）ENFJ在模型家族和精度间保持主导地位；（2）4-bit量化大致保留粗粒度人格结构，而2-bit量化破坏细粒度提示一致性和跨精度一致性；（3）人格决策在高层涌现，早期层存在显著歧义；（4）推理解码可改变人格，而人格对齐的条件化可提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM的人格不是静态属性，而是量化和推理策略共同塑造的动态、逐层涌现的决策过程。研究强调了对量化LLM内部动态和推理策略进行考虑的重要性，为人格敏感型聊天机器人应用的可靠性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Personality is increasingly important in large language models (LLMs), as it shapes users' trust, engagement, and emotional experiences. While the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI) has emerged as a common framework for assessing LLMs' personality, existing studies focus primarily on full-precision models and evaluate only final outputs. They overlook the widespread deployment of quantized LLMs requiring low memory footprints, whose personality traits remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic MBTI analysis of open-source LLMs across multiple precisions, including mainstream 4-bit methods (GPTQ, AWQ) and extreme 2-bit settings (AQLM variants). Beyond output-level evaluation, we examine how personality emerges across layers through option-level entropy and confidence-gap dynamics, and introduce Uncertainty-Amplified Layer Decoding (UALD) to study decoding-induced personality drift at inference time. Our results reveal a key insight: LLMs' personality is not a static property, but an emergent, layer-dependent decision process sensitive to quantization, prompting, and decoding. Specifically, we find that (1) ENFJ remains dominant across model families and precisions; (2) 4-bit quantization largely preserves coarse personality structure, while 2-bit quantization disrupts fine-grained prompt consistency and cross-precision agreement; (3) personality decisions emerges in upper layers, following substantial ambiguity in early layers; and (4) inference decoding can shift personality, while personality-aligned conditioning improves robustness. These findings provide a new perspective on the behavioral reliability of quantized LLMs and highlight the importance of considering internal dynamics and inference strategies in personality-sensitive chatbot applications.

</details>


### [155] [Distinct dynamics of conceptual and referential disruptions in human reading and large language model processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25999)
*Rui He,Nihal Altay,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过干扰短叙事中的概念信息与指称信息，对比了人类自定步速阅读与大语言模型的预测/表征处理动态，发现概念性干扰引发强而局部的处理代价（在目标词后立即出现并快速衰减），而指称性干扰引发更弱、更渐进的跨词衰减效应，且受句子边界调节更强，证明两类意义具有不同的加工动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言意义根植于概念内容，而指称特定实体的能力在话语进程中浮现。一个核心问题是：概念维度与指称维度的意义加工是否具有不同的认知与计算动态？理解这两类维度的处理差异有助于揭示语言理解的认知架构以及神经语言模型如何处理意义。

Method: 在短叙事中分别选择性干扰概念信息或指称信息，然后追踪：（1）人类自定步速阅读行为中的处理代价模式；（2）大语言模型中的预测处理（语境surprisal）与表征处理（输出层表示）的扰动模式。通过对比两种干扰类型在不同位置（目标词、后续词）和不同边界（句子边界）条件下的效应大小与衰减模式来进行分析。

Result: 人类阅读中，概念干扰产生强而局部的处理代价，立即出现、快速达峰并急剧衰减；指称干扰效应更弱、衰减更缓慢，且受句子边界调节更强。语言模型的语境surprisal模式与人类阅读高度相似（概念干扰更大且更局部，指称干扰更小且更渐进），但输出层表征中指称干扰产生更大的初始偏移，两种干扰随后均呈幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 两类意义具有可区分的处理动态：概念信息施加更局部集中的整合代价，而指称信息涉及维持话语级身份的分布式加工过程。语言模型在预测层与人类行为高度一致，但表征层展示了不同的模式，提示预测与表征维度可能编码不同类型的语义信息。

Abstract: Linguistic meaning is grounded in conceptual content, from which reference to particular entities emerges as words enter discourse. To examine the processing dynamics associated with these two dimensions of meaning, we selectively disrupted conceptual or referential information in short narratives and traced the resulting effects in human self-paced reading and in the predictive and representational processing of large language models. In human reading, conceptual disruptions produced a strong but localized processing cost, emerging immediately after the distorted word, reaching an early maximum, and then declining rapidly. Referential disruptions produced weaker effects, which decreased more gradually across subsequent words, and were more strongly modulated by sentence boundaries. In the language model, both disruptions emerged immediately at the manipulated word. Contextual model surprisal showed a pattern closely paralleling human reading: conceptual disruption produced a larger, more locally concentrated effect that decayed rapidly, whereas referential disruption produced a smaller and more gradual downstream effect. Output-layer representations showed a different pattern: referential disruption produced a larger initial displacement, while both distortions were subsequently characterized by power-law decay. Together, these results provide convergent evidence for distinguishable processing dynamics of two types of meaning: conceptual information imposes a more locally concentrated integration cost, whereas referential information engages a more distributed process of maintaining discourse-level identity.

</details>


### [156] [VISA: Agentic Self-Evolving Data Synthesis for Multimodal Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26013)
*Min Zeng,Guanxin Tan,Libin Cen,Yawei Wen,Rui Hu,Liuyang Bian,Xiaolong Chen,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VISA（Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent）框架，将多模态指令合成从一次性生成-过滤范式重构为自进化的闭环流程。VISA在每个轮次中分析图像以筛选约束、从持久内存中采样多样性和难度感知的约束集、生成候选指令，并用可执行工具和结构化LLM裁判验证样本。失败样本触发诊断导向恢复，成功样本对目标模型进行探测以估计难度，验证信号和目标模型失败档案写回内存，使后续轮次自适应扩展约束空间。同一验证器契约还可为强化学习提供奖励信号，无需单独训练奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态指令合成流程通常采用单向生成-过滤范式，丢弃了失败样本反馈、验证器结果和目标模型错误等有价值信息，导致训练数据的准确性、多样性、可验证性和挑战性受限。

Method: VISA采用agentic自进化循环框架：（1）每轮分析图像，过滤不兼容约束并发现新的可验证约束；（2）从持久内存采样多样性和难度感知的约束集；（3）生成候选指令；（4）用可执行工具和结构化LLM裁判验证样本；（5）失败样本通过诊断导向恢复，成功样本对目标模型探测以估计难度；（6）验证器信号和目标模型失败档案写回内存，驱动后续轮次自适应扩展约束空间、减少模板重复并聚焦未解决弱点。

Result: 在MM-IFEval基准上，VISA相比强基线一致地提升多模态指令跟随能力，同时在7个公共基准上保持一般多模态能力不退化。

Conclusion: VISA通过将反馈信号闭环回写内存的自进化机制，有效提升多模态指令跟随训练数据的质量与难度，并且验证器契约可复用为强化学习奖励信号，无需单独训练奖励模型，展示了agentic数据合成框架的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction-following models require training data that is accurate, diverse, verifiable, and challenging. Existing synthesis pipelines typically follow a one-pass generate-and-filter paradigm, discarding feedback from failed samples, verifier outcomes, and target-model errors. We present VISA (Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent), an agentic framework that reformulates multimodal instruction synthesis as a self-evolving loop. At each round, VISA analyzes an image to filter incompatible constraints and discover new verifiable ones, samples diversity- and difficulty-aware constraint sets from persistent memory, generates candidate instructions, and verifies the resulting samples with executable tools and structured large language model judges. Failed samples trigger diagnostic-guided recovery, while accepted samples are probed against the target model to estimate difficulty. The resulting verifier signals and target-model failure profiles are written back to memory, allowing subsequent rounds to adaptively expand the constraint space, reduce template repetition, and focus on unresolved model weaknesses. The same verifier contracts further provide reward signals for reinforcement learning without a separately trained reward model. Experiments on MM-IFEval show that VISA consistently improves multimodal instruction following over strong baselines, while preserving general multimodal capability across seven public benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Local Surprise: Grounded Dialogue as Selective Belief Revision under Referential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26035)
*Ziming Liu,Bhanu Chaitanya Jasti,Ziyang Xu,Hongyu Wu,Yi Wu,Jiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控的、数据驱动的对话框架，用于逐轮判断听者在对话中应保留还是修订其当前理解。通过比较四种基于理论的修订策略，发现仅依赖局部不匹配的更新策略会破坏语义基础并降低检索性能，而基于累积不确定性的策略能在保持理解一致性的同时维持强检索性能。关键反直觉发现是：局部不匹配促进保留，而累积不确定性促进修订，这符合"概念契约"理论的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言系统将局部不匹配视为需要更新理解的线索（分歧即调整），但对话理解可能更保守——听者倾向于参考先前理解来解释不匹配证据，而非立即修订。现有研究缺乏在受控条件下比较不同修订策略的系统框架。

Method: 引入一个受控的数据驱动框架，用于对话中逐轮的保留/修订决策。在完全相同的条件下学习四种竞争性修订策略，每种策略反映不同的关于听者何时应保留或修订理解的假设。其中包含不匹配驱动策略（仅基于局部分歧更新）和不确定性敏感策略（在不匹配基础上扩展累积证据）。

Result: 两项主要发现：(1) 不匹配驱动策略对不匹配反应强烈，但会破坏语义基础的稳定性并降低检索性能；(2) 不确定性敏感策略通过累积证据扩展不匹配更新，在保持理解连贯性的同时维持强检索性能。反直觉模式涌现：局部不匹配促进保留，累积不确定性促进修订。

Conclusion: 对话理解中，听者倾向于在局部不匹配时维持先前理解，只有当不确定性充分积累后才进行修订。这一模式与概念契约理论一致，表明基于不确定性的保守更新策略优于基于局部分歧的快速反应策略。

Abstract: When a speaker refers to a scene that the listener cannot directly see, the listener must decide whether to preserve its current understanding or revise it as new utterances arrive. Many language systems treat local mismatch as a cue for updating: divergence from the current understanding encourages adjustment. Yet conversational understanding may be more conservative, interpreting mismatching evidence relative to prior understanding rather than immediately revising it. We introduce a controlled, data-driven framework for turn-by-turn preserve/revise decisions in dialogue, where competing revision policies are learned under otherwise identical conditions. We compare four theory-driven revision strategies, each reflecting a different assumption about when listeners should preserve or revise. Two findings stand out. First, a mismatch-driven policy that updates solely based on local divergence reacts strongly to mismatch but destabilizes grounding and degrades retrieval. Second, an uncertainty-sensitive policy extends mismatch-based updating with accumulated evidence, preserving coherent understanding while maintaining strong retrieval performance. Surprisingly, coherent understanding emerges from a counterintuitive pattern: local mismatch promotes preservation, whereas accumulated uncertainty promotes revision, suggesting that listeners maintain prior understanding despite local mismatch and revise only when uncertainty sufficiently accumulates. This pattern is consistent with conceptual pact theory.

</details>


### [158] [Fine-Tuning Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26060)
*Leonardo Duart,Tiago Fonseca,Thiago Chacón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究初步探索了将Whisper多语言基础模型微调应用于巴尼瓦语（一种巴西亚马孙地区的阿劳瓦克语系原住民语言）的自动语音识别。使用来自语言文档项目的1,373条人工转录录音（约0.54小时）进行监督微调，最佳模型达到37.5%的词错误率和7.45%的字错误率，证明多语言基础模型可有效适配极低资源原住民语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言基础模型推动了ASR的显著进步，但大多数进展集中在高资源语言上，原住民语言仍面临语音资源和语言技术匮乏的困境。本研究旨在探索将Whisper等通用模型适配到巴尼瓦语这一极低资源原住民语言的可行性。

Method: 使用语言学文档项目中的1,373条人工转录录音（约0.54小时语音，主要由孤立词和简短诱导话语组成）构建语料库，对Whisper Small模型进行有监督微调，并以词错误率（WER）和字错误率（CER）作为评估指标。

Result: 最佳微调模型取得了37.5%的WER和7.45%的CER，表明Whisper能够成功适配到该极低资源原住民语言，并为巴尼瓦语ASR建立了初步基准。

Conclusion: 自适多语言基础模型可以有效支持极低资源原住民语言的语音识别任务。该结果为未来使用更大数据集、语言专属适配策略和后处理技术的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) technologies have achieved remarkable performance in recent years through the use of large multilingual foundation models. However, most advances remain concentrated on high-resource languages, while indigenous languages continue to suffer from a lack of speech resources and language technologies. This work presents a preliminary study on the adaptation of Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa, an indigenous Arawakan language spoken in Brazil, Colombia, and Venezuela. The experiments were conducted using a corpus of 1,373 manually transcribed recordings obtained from a linguistic documentation project. The corpus contains approximately 0.54 hours of speech and consists primarily of isolated words and short elicited utterances. The Whisper Small model was fine-tuned using supervised learning and evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER). The best model achieved a WER of 37.5% and a CER of 7.45%, demonstrating that multilingual foundation models can be successfully adapted to extremely low-resource indigenous languages. The results establish an initial baseline for Baniwa Automatic Speech Recognition and provide a foundation for future research involving larger datasets, language-specific adaptation strategies, and post-processing techniques.

</details>


### [159] [Prefix Sliding for efficient test-time scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26070)
*Niklas Muennighoff,Zhengyang Wang,Zeyi Chen,Weijia Shi,Binyuan Hui,John Yang,Dapeng Jiang,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar,Ludwig Schmidt,Percy Liang,Jason Wei,Andrew Y. Ng,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Prefix Sliding方法，在推理过程中丢弃不重要的中间token，仅保留前缀（指令和工具）和最近几千个token（当前推理），将内存需求限制为常数，实现高效的长时测试时缩放，无需训练即可提速3倍，结合强化学习训练还可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放（test-time scaling）依赖模型进行更长的推理以提升性能，但模型通过全注意力将所有推理轨迹保留在内存中，导致需要长思考的困难任务计算开销过大。作者发现大部分中间推理token在推理继续时重要性下降，质疑保留它们的必要性。

Method: 提出Prefix Sliding方法，在推理过程中丢弃不属于前缀（包含关键指令和工具）或最近几千个token窗口的中间推理token。这使得总内存需求不受推理长度影响，从而实现高效的长时测试时缩放。方法可无需训练直接应用，也可结合强化学习进行训练以支持超过十万token的推理轨迹。

Result: 无需训练时，Prefix Sliding可使现有模型推理提速3倍且保持性能；结合强化学习训练后，可通过扩展至超过十万token的推理轨迹获得更好的性能。消融实验表明Prefix Sliding优于对中间token进行摘要或使用普通滑动窗口的方法。

Conclusion: Prefix Sliding通过有选择地丢弃不重要的中间推理token，在控制内存开销的同时维持甚至提升模型性能，为长时测试时缩放提供了一种高效且无需额外训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling uses extra test-time compute to improve performance, such as letting language models reason longer when solving a problem. As models keep the entire reasoning trace in memory via full attention, hard tasks that need long thinking can be prohibitively expensive. However, we find most intermediate reasoning tokens lose importance as the model continues reasoning. This calls into question whether retaining them is worth the cost. Based on this insight, we propose Prefix Sliding, which discards tokens during reasoning that are not part of the prefix or the window of the last few thousand tokens. The prefix has key instructions and tools available to the model, while the most recent tokens are the current reasoning the model is working on. This caps the total memory requirement regardless of how long the model reasons, allowing for efficient long-horizon test-time scaling. Without training, Prefix Sliding can make existing models 3x faster while maintaining performance. Training with Prefix Sliding using reinforcement learning can achieve better performance by enabling scaling to reasoning traces beyond a hundred thousand tokens. Ablations show Prefix Sliding outperforms summarizing intermediate tokens or vanilla sliding window. Our code is at https://github.com/Muennighoff/prefix-sliding

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Sample Complexity of the Second-Best Bilateral Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25303)
*Qiaoyun Shi,Shengxin Liu,Zongqi Wan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究学习近似最优双边交易机制的样本复杂度，重点关注满足贝叶斯激励相容（BIC）、临时个体理性（IIR）和事前弱预算平衡（WBB）的机制，目标是实现次优交易收益（second-best gains-from-trade）基准。作者在三种场景下给出了匹配或近乎匹配的上下界：规则乘积分布下加性ε近似的样本复杂度为Θ̃(h²/ε²)；乘法(1-α)近似下为基准敏感的Θ̃(h/(SB(D)α²))；无界分布（单调风险率假设）下的复杂度取决于比值χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/SB(D)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于双边交易机制学习的文献聚焦于简单机制或固定价格机制，而本文关注满足BIC、IIR和WBB的更一般机制类，目标是设计基于样本的机制以达到次优交易收益基准，从而填补了这一理论空白。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了三种不同场景下的样本复杂度：1) 规则乘积分布在[0,h]²上的加性ε近似；2) 相同假设下的乘法(1-α)近似；3) 单调风险率（MHR）假设下的无界分布。通过构造匹配的上下界来完成分析，并引入基准敏感量（如SB(D)和χ_μ(D)）来刻画复杂度。

Result: 在规则乘积分布的加性ε近似下，样本复杂度为Θ̃(h²/ε²)；在乘法(1-α)近似下，样本复杂度为Θ̃(h/(SB(D)α²))，对次优交易收益基准有不可避免的依赖；在无界MHR分布下，复杂度依赖于比值χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/SB(D)。三组结果均为匹配或近乎匹配的上下界。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了不同近似目标和分布假设下学习双边交易机制的样本复杂度特征，特别是乘法近似的基准敏感性和无界分布下的比率依赖，为机制设计中的样本效率研究提供了系统性结论。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning near-optimal bilateral trade mechanisms. Unlike previous work on learning simple or fixed-price bilateral-trade mechanisms, we focus on mechanisms satisfying Bayesian incentive compatibility (BIC), interim individual rationality (IIR), and ex-ante weak budget balance (WBB). In other words, our target is to design a sample-based mechanism that achieves the second-best gains-from-trade benchmark.
  We give matching or nearly matching upper and lower bounds in three regimes. For regular product distributions on $[0,h]^2$, additive $\varepsilon$-approximation has sample complexity $\widetildeΘ(h^2/\varepsilon^2)$. For multiplicative $(1-α)$-approximation under the same assumptions, we find that the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(h/(\mathrm{SB}(D)α^2))$, which is benchmark-sensitive with unavoidable dependence on the second-best gains from trade $\mathrm{SB}(D)$. We also investigate unbounded distributions under a monotone hazard rate (MHR) assumption. The sample complexity depends on the ratio $χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/\mathrm{SB}(D)$, where $μ(D)$ is the sum of the buyer's expected value and the seller's expected cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型(VLM)的自动化多粒度图构建方法，用于从建筑平面图图像中提取结构化布局知识。通过定义Level-of-Graphs(LoGs)框架，实现了节点识别、边推理、文本解析和图粗化四个步骤的自动化。以147个学术图书馆平面图为案例，验证了VLM生成的图与人工标注高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%），并展示了不同粒度图在不同任务中的优势：中粒度图在节点级区域预测中表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647），粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中最有效（Spearman系数=0.610）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图图像蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，可用于设计检索、基于知识的推理和BIM增广，但针对公共建筑自动构建任务自适应图表示仍具挑战性。现有方法难以实现可扩展、免标注的结构化信息提取。

Method: 首先定义了面向公共建筑的多粒度Level-of-Graphs(LoGs)框架；提出基于VLM的自动LoG构建方法，包括四个关键步骤：节点识别(Node Identification)、边推理(Edge Inference)、文本解析(Text Parsing)和图粗化(Graph Coarsening)；使用147个全球学术图书馆平面图作为案例进行系统评估。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注图高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%），生成三个粒度图每张平面图耗时509.3秒。中粒度图在节点级区域预测中表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647，复杂度仅为细粒度的65%）；粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中最有效（Spearman相关系数=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度的16%）。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将平面图图像转换为知识表示，实现了可扩展、免标注的结构化布局信息提取，推动了设计信息学的发展，增强了设计信息在建筑全生命周期中的利用率。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [162] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMGUIDE方法，将用户上下文建模为类型化、领域特定的"Sim"模块，并用程序化示例进行约束扎根（grounding），显著优于基于检索（RAG）的个性化方案。同时构建SIMBENCH基准（47个偏好条件规划任务），并在100个τ-bench任务上验证其有效性。核心结论是：用户表征的格式（而非内容）是首要设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI智能体将用户视为单一实体，使用扁平化档案进行提示拼接，无法处理同一用户在不同生活场景中持有不同甚至冲突优先级的问题。作者指出核心问题不在于智能体缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表征的格式决定了智能体能否有效利用这些信息。

Method: （1）SIMGUIDE：将用户上下文结构化为类型化、领域特定的块（Sims），每个约束用来自历史决策的程序化示例进行扎根。（2）SIMBENCH：构建47个偏好条件规划任务的诊断套件，正确计划依赖于当前激活的用户上下文。（3）对比实验：与声明式Sim约束和RAG个性化进行对比。（4）参数层面：进行任务匹配的LoRA微调，并按Sim类型而非用户身份进行adapter路由。

Result: 声明式Sim约束本身未能超越RAG，但程序化扎根的Sim在GPT-4o上超越RAG（+7.9 Preference Adherence点，p=0.013），并在100个τ-bench任务上于GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.5上均复现该优势（p≤0.023）。参数层面：任务匹配的LoRA微调比未适配基座模型提升12.8 ROUGE-L点，按Sim类型而非用户身份路由adapter再增加7.3点，且对28%的路由错误具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 表征格式（而非表征内容）是第一阶设计变量。将用户上下文按类型化和程序化扎根的结构化方式组织，比单纯增加用户信息量更能决定个性化智能体的性能上限。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [163] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE)的混合架构，将大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链中的端到端决策（需求预测、库存优化、运输路线规划和中断缓解），以实现更智能、更安全和更可扩展的供应链决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则和纯优化的供应链系统难以应对当前供应链的高不确定性、动态性和易中断性；同时，日益丰富的异质数据源（如事务性需求信号和非结构化中断报告）为构建能够同时推理、适应和优化的智能系统提供了机会。

Method: 提出LLM-DE混合架构，它将大语言模型的语义推理能力与数学优化、概率预测以及安全约束决策过滤器相结合，形成一个完整的端到端决策流水线，涵盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解等环节。

Result: 实验发现表明，语言推理与优化及形式化约束相结合，能够产生更智能、更安全且更可扩展的供应链决策，验证了LLM-DE架构相对于纯数据驱动或启发式方案的优越性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新架构、完整的算法流水线以及基于数学构造的操作决策系统形式化框架，为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了既务实又具理论基础的实施方案，能够在不确定性和大规模复杂性条件下可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [164] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2017-2025年间52项关于AI心理聊天机器人的实证研究，评估其文化、语言和基础设施对非洲语境的适配性，并提出文化适应型数字心理健康（CADMH）框架以指导非洲AI心理支持工具的本土化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康问题因基础设施不足、污名化及专业人员短缺而长期未得到充分解决。AI聊天机器人被视为低成本、高可及性的潜在解决方案，但现有研究对非洲特定文化语境的适配性缺乏系统评估，亟需填补这一知识空白。

Method: 作者对2017年至2025年间发表的52项实证研究进行了系统性综述，从文化、语言和基础设施三个维度批判性分析AI聊天机器人在非洲语境中的适用性，并基于研究发现开发了CADMH框架，整合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化及伦理保障。

Result: 综述表明AI聊天机器人能够提升心理支持的可及性、减轻焦虑和抑郁症状、扩展社会心理支持范围，但绝大多数系统仍基于西方模型和英语设计，缺乏针对非洲的本土化适配，在本地相关性、包容性和可持续性方面存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: 研究提出了CADMH框架作为文化适应性的指导方案，强调了AI聊天机器人在非洲医疗体系中整合的机遇与障碍，为研究、实践和政策制定提供了方向性建议。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [165] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了近端量子硬件上二元量子分类器的测量预算分配问题，即如何在训练态数量n和每个态的测量次数S之间进行权衡。作者证明了将有限样本项（~√(d/n)）与有限射击项（~√((log n)/S)）分离的分布无关泛化界，并在B=nS约束下给出了保守的闭式分配规则：n* = 2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))，S* = B/n*，达到B^{-1/4}的最坏情况收敛率。通过PennyLane仿真在2比特和4比特变分量子电路上验证了理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近端量子硬件上估计Born概率需要重复电路执行，而量子学习实验受固定测量预算B的限制，必须在不同训练态数量n和每态测量次数S之间做出权衡。现有理论大多假设测量预算无限或已知精确的期望值，但实际硬件上有限射击的统计噪声是不可避免的。因此需要一套能够在有限测量预算下指导实验设计的统计原则。

Method: 作者针对具有固定或独立选择的测量算子M的二元量子分类器，证明了将有限样本项与有限射击项分离的分布无关泛化界。在B=nS约束下，通过最小化该界的保守闭式替身得到分配规则n* = 2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))和S* = B/n*。该替身与精确最小化器具有相同的渐近标度，最坏情况速率为B^{-1/4}。理论部分辅以PennyLane平台上2比特和4比特变分量子电路在九个合成二元分类基准上的仿真验证。

Result: 在所有测试配置中，单侧经验泛化差距均低于理论界，验证了该保守界的有效性。得到的分配规则为有限射击评估和实验前规划提供了统计指导，其最坏情况收敛率为B^{-1/4}。

Conclusion: 该工作为近端量子学习系统的测量预算分配提供了保守的统计准则，补充了硬件级调度和电路设计方面的考虑。将保证扩展到完全自适应的射击噪声训练仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [166] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用架构，利用大语言模型（LLM）将海事信息交换模型（MIEM）和Rich Semantic Track模型变得可行：操作员以自然语言输入观测，LLM将其翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SARs），知识图谱累积SARs，第二遍LLM执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义轨道模型在国防和执法领域的采用障碍在于，操作员需要学习形式化本体语言并手动将观测编码为类型化逻辑断言，这严重阻碍了实际部署与应用。

Method: 1) 操作员用自然语言提供观测；2) 第一遍LLM将自然语言翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SARs），即命名案例框架（case frames），在单一紧凑结构中捕获n元关系；3) 知识图谱累积SARs；4) 第二遍LLM基于图谱执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。该架构将语义轨道表示与神经流形几何联系起来，提供了理论基础。

Result: 通过两个详细示例（9/11时代袭击前兆指标场景和海上海运货物检查场景）展示了从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整流水线，证明该架构可立即用当前技术部署。

Conclusion: 该架构使Track Model和MIEM可立即部署，建立了针对专有封闭方法的现有技术（prior art），并将方法置于连接语义轨道表示与神经流形几何的理论框架中。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [167] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对CS2等FPS游戏中的账号完整性审查问题，提出将"当前行为是否与历史操作者一致"的形式化为同玩家验证任务。通过从比赛中提取玩家的行为指纹（涵盖准星控制、运动-停火协调、经济/购买、战斗/交战、时间节奏等），训练模型判断两个行为观测是否来自同一真实玩家。基于1,330个CS2 demo提取13,300个观测，构造663,590对训练样本，最终模型达到平均ROC AUC 0.931，在95%精确率下实现0.722的不同玩家召回率。特征分析显示低层操作（准星控制、开火节奏、运动-停火协调）是最强的身份信号。通过多demo历史聚合，K=10时AUC从0.931提升至0.986。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CS2等FPS游戏中，账号完整性审查需要判断账号近期行为是否与历史操作者一致，这涉及临时换人、段位代练、高技能玩家使用低段位账号等情况。人工审查需要将当前比赛与多场历史比赛对比，工作量大且效率低。因此需要自动化方法来建模玩家身份一致性问题。

Method: 将审查任务形式化为同玩家验证任务：从比赛回放（demo）中提取单玩家的行为轨迹编码为demo-玩家行为指纹，训练模型判断两个行为观测是否来自同一真实玩家。指纹基于CS2游戏理解覆盖五个维度：准星控制、运动-停火协调、经济/购买、战斗/交战和时间节奏。从1,330个CS2 demo提取13,300个demo-玩家观测，从8,840万候选对空间中采样663,590个同/不同玩家对用于监督训练和评估。同时进行多demo历史聚合评估。

Result: 最终成对模型达到平均ROC AUC 0.931，在95%精确率下实现0.722不同玩家召回率。特征分析表明最强的身份信号来自低层操作，尤其是准星控制、开火节奏和运动-停火协调。在账号历史聚合评估中，增加历史深度使AUC从K=1单对基线的0.931提升到K=10的0.986。

Conclusion: CS2 demo行为可以支持监督式同玩家验证和通过多demo历史聚合的账号级身份一致性建模。稳定的低层机械习惯比单场性能结果更具信息量，这一发现验证了基于行为指纹的自动化账号完整性审查方案的可行性。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [168] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向工业数字线程的开放语义图统一框架，通过本体驱动的RDF知识图谱，将来自11个模拟源的异构制造数据（ERP、MES、PLM等）跨9个领域进行统一，并借助自动发现引擎和LLM Agent访问层实现跨系统洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA等多套异构系统，各自拥有独立的数据模型和API，形成数据孤岛，阻碍了整体分析、根因调查和工业4.0的可追溯性。点对点集成复杂度为O(n^2)且产生脆弱的依赖关系，亟需一种统一的语义化集成框架。

Method: 采用五阶段ETL管道构建本体驱动的RDF知识图谱，集成11个模拟源、9个领域的数据，涵盖78个RDFS类、108个对象属性、243个数据属性，参考ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、AAS、RAMI 4.0等标准；自动化实体消解生成97条owl:sameAs链接；自动发现引擎实施九类策略（跨站相关性、报警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等）；通过287个Model Context Protocol工具将图暴露给LLM Agent作为SPARQL原生的语义层。

Result: 阻断24个跨系统工具后召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接；留一消融实验证实九种策略中六种贡献了独特信号；针对65个信号清单（16正例、49空例）的验证达到F1=1.00（95% Clopper-Pearson置信区间[0.79, 1.00]）；五个行业模板（航空航天、CPG、制药、医疗器械、涡轮叶片）展示了跨垂直领域的模式稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了语义图统一可有效打破制造数据孤岛，显著提升了跨系统信号的可发现性，并通过标准化本体和可扩展架构展示了跨行业应用的可行性。作者也坦诚指出清单由作者构建，验证结果应视为内部验证而非独立验证。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads.
