Conclusion: 强化学习能够教会7B参数的小型模型以专业气象学家风格撰写文本并忠实解读AI天气数据，显著提升生成式气象文档的质量与可靠性，为气象预报传播提供了更安全的AI辅助途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [234] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经代理模型在无导数优化中效果矛盾的根源，指出角色（Role）、半径（Radius）和空间（Room）三个因素才是决定学习型局部模型是否有价值的关键，而非训练曲线所报告的拟合精度。通过大量基准实验验证，守护式辅助（safeguarded assistance）能提升求解精度，而梯度替代会降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中神经代理模型在无导数优化中的效果相互矛盾：同一类模型能减少一个求解器的评估次数，却对另一个求解器无效甚至更差。作者希望解释并消解这一矛盾，找出真正决定学习型局部模型能否带来收益的因素。

Method: 作者提出了三个决定性因素：（1）角色——代理模型仅提出候选解由真实目标函数审批则有益，若替代求解器所依赖的梯度则有害；（2）半径——拟合于优化轨迹的模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，误差不会随邻域缩小而消失，也不随邻域增长而保持；（3）空间——代理只能加速基方法尚有能力取得的进展。作者形式化了半径感知的局部泛化（radius-aware local generalisation），并将其与经典的全线性条件（fully linear condition）建立联系。

Result: 在117个基准实例上：守护式辅助使高精度求解数从67提升至84，而梯度替代使其降至65；从训练损失中移除梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148；1000组配对比较跨越十个噪声水平显示不存在噪声阈值，只有提前停止的基方法才是关键。附加同样的代理后，模型信赖域求解器从88降至86（因空间有限），发布的插值软件保持在103。在蒙特卡洛库存模型上，修复接受接口价值10.40成本单位，而代理本身价值为0.00。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型在无导数优化中的价值由角色、半径和空间三个因素决定，而非拟合精度。代理应作为守护式辅助而非替换梯度；其可靠性受限于有界邻域；且只能加速基方法本身尚能取得的进展。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [235] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息误差场学习（PIEFL）框架，通过在PINNs训练后期引入辅助误差网络，将学习目标从解场转移到误差场，在相同计算预算下实现更高的求解精度，且无需修改主网络结构，可作为通用后训练优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练后期，继续优化主网络往往只能带来微小的精度提升，却消耗大量计算资源，存在"边际效益递减"的问题，需要一种更高效的后期优化策略。

Method: PIEFL框架包含两个阶段：（1）主网络先训练至满意的精度；（2）引入辅助误差网络，通过推导物理约束下的误差控制方程，让误差网络学习当前近似解与精确解之间的差异，将学到的误差修正与主网络预测结合以提升精度。该方法无需修改主网络架构，可兼容现有PINN模型。

Result: 在代表性PDE上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，PIEFL相比传统PINN方法实现了更高的求解精度，验证了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: PIEFL通过聚焦于修正已有预测误差而非在整个解空间上持续优化，避免了后期训练的计算浪费，为提升PINN性能提供了一种简单有效且具有广泛兼容性的后训练优化途径。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [236] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REP-LIE，一种在微调过程中进行资源高效剪枝的新方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计权重重要性，无需全梯度计算和全参数优化，在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型上均取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer预训练语言模型的计算和内存成本高昂，部署困难；传统剪枝方法依赖基于梯度的全量重要性估计且需要预先微调模型，资源消耗不可接受。作者旨在提出一种在微调过程中同时进行剪枝的方法，避免繁琐的预训练和全参数优化。

Method: REP-LIE利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计模型权重的重要性，避免全梯度计算；引入稳定性分数（stability score）应对重要性估计中的固有随机性，并基于此分数对不重要的模型参数进行迭代剪枝；剪枝后的模型通过轻量级更新进一步微调，无需全参数优化。

Result: 在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上的大量实验表明，REP-LIE与传统方法相比仍能达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE通过结合LoRA梯度和稳定性分数，在微调过程中实现资源高效的模型剪枝，在显著降低资源消耗的同时保持模型性能，为资源受限环境下的大模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [237] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于自回归Transformer的AI干扰抑制方法基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层，以提高射频信号中结构化干扰（如数字电视OFDM信号）的抑制性能，同时通过推理优化技术降低延迟，实验证明在低延迟下相比传统方法和先前AI方法实现了更强的干扰抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统干扰抑制方法性能有限，而AI方法通过联合考虑有用信号和混合信号可以超越传统方法。然而，现有AI方法在延迟方面可能不满足实际应用需求，因此需要在保持低延迟的同时进一步提升干扰抑制性能。

Method: 基于先前使用自回归Transformer模型的方法，添加有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层；同时实验多种推理优化技术以在不显著损失精度的前提下加速推理。实验中，有用信号为数字调制射频信号，结构化干扰为数字电视信号（常见OFDM传输类型）。

Result: 所提出的方法在低延迟下实现了优于传统方法和先前AI方法的干扰抑制效果。通过PESQ等音频指标验证了AI方法的优势，并展示了该方法在多种操作相关场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 结合FSQ分词器层的自回归Transformer方法能在低延迟约束下有效提升结构化干扰抑制性能，并通过推理优化技术在精度与速度之间取得了良好平衡，具有实际操作场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [238] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSR-IVA（掩码结构残差独立向量分析）框架，用于在sMRI与动态功能网络连接（dFNC）的多模态融合中，结合共享结构表征与状态特异性残差适应，并处理受试者未表达所有动态状态的情况。在ADNI队列上，MSR-IVA相比独立成对IVA基线将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，不匹配依赖降低15.7%，并在双状态受试者中实现0.9177的结构源相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构-功能多模态融合方法存在两个关键问题：一是将同一结构潜表征耦合多个动态功能状态时，对每个状态独立应用IVA会产生不相关的结构分解，而强制完全相同的分解又会抑制状态特异的关联；二是并非每个受试者都表达所有动态状态，需要对这种不完整的状态表达进行建模。

Method: 提出MSR-IVA（masked structural residual IVA）框架，它结合了共享结构表征与状态特异性残差适应，并通过掩码机制处理不完整的状态表达。该方法在共享结构成分和状态特异残差之间取得平衡，既保留源对应关系又允许状态特异的调整。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病神经影像计划（ADNI）队列上，MSR-IVA相比独立成对IVA基线将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，不匹配依赖降低15.7%。在同时表达两种状态的受试者中，MSR-IVA的跨状态结构源平均绝对相关为0.9177，而无共享时为0.2978，说明该方法实现了受控的结构共享，在保留源对应的同时允许状态特异适应。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA通过状态感知的结构共享与残差适应机制，有效解决了多状态结构-功能融合中结构分解不一致与状态表达不完整的问题，在提高源耦合和降低不匹配依赖的同时保持了状态特异的灵活性。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [239] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D³-MOPD，一种零开销的动态域调度器，利用训练中已有的逐域逆向KL散度信号在线调整域数据混合比例，解决多教师on-policy蒸馏中固定数据混合忽略不同域收敛速度差异的问题，在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型上关闭了97%的学生-教师性能差距（对比vanilla MOPD的63%），并以约3倍少的rollout步骤达到相同峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOPD方法在训练前固定逐域数据混合比例，但不同域收敛速度差异显著：部分域早早收敛而其他域在训练预算内持续提升。固定混合比例导致计算资源浪费在快速收敛的域上，同时欠训练慢速收敛的域。

Method: D³-MOPD采用训练进程外的异步监视器，周期性跟踪每个域的KL轨迹，估计剩余提升空间和当前改进速率，据此动态调整域采样比例。该方法利用训练过程中已经产生的逐域逆向KL信号，不改变核心训练循环，实现零额外开销。可自然扩展到任意数量的域。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型上，D³-MOPD关闭了97%的平均学生-教师性能差距（vanilla MOPD为63%），以约3倍的rollout步骤缩减达到相同峰值性能，并在七个基准中的三个上超越领域专家教师。

Conclusion: 动态调度域混合比例能够显著提升多教师on-policy蒸馏的效率与最终性能，且随着域数量增加，更多样化的收敛模式为调度器提供更大的优化空间，预期收益也随之增长。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [240] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)，通过在训练时让潜在世界模型进行自由运行的rollout，并对其解码结果与真实观测计算重建误差，弥补了训练与部署之间的分布差距。在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87提高到6.97时间单位（提升1.80倍），且不增加参数、仅增加训练计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型（latent world model）的训练使用锚定在观测上的潜在变量来训练解码器，但在部署时，解码器需要处理模型自身自由运行数百步后产生的潜在变量，这导致了训练与推理之间的分布偏移（distribution shift）。这种gap损害了模型长期预测的有效性。

Method: RDR通过在训练过程中让模型以与评估完全相同的方式自由运行（free-run），对每个rollout步骤的潜在变量进行解码，并计算解码结果与真实地面真值之间的重建误差，作为额外的损失项。该损失项不引入新参数，仅在训练时产生计算开销，且当权重设为零时可退化为标准训练目标，因此所有对比均为单标志A/B实验。

Result: 在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提升至6.97±0.42时间单位（1.80倍提升），参数数量保持不变（193,568个）。该改进在未见过的随机种子（never-used seeds）上得到确认，并在10/10个预注册配置中均在1.71-2.50倍区间内保持优势。

Conclusion: RDR是一种简单有效、零额外参数的方法，通过在训练中模拟部署时的自由运行条件，显著提升了潜在世界模型的长期预测能力。作者指出目前结果仅来自单一系统，潜在宽度增大时优势增长的扫描仅是描述性的，两个经典任务上的对照实验仍属初步，结论的普适性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [241] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究标准神经架构在动态规划（DP）目标上无法泛化到更长输入的原因。每个有限min-plus DP可被视为DAG上的最短路径，等价于一个热带多项式，其扩展牛顿多面体编码了路径获胜的决策边界。作者证明图、多项式、多面体三种描述在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面构成同构半环，并提出两个结构性负结果解释长度泛化困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经架构在动态规划目标上对更长输入的长度泛化常常失败，作者希望从几何角度理解这一困难的本质原因。通过将DP问题映射为热带多项式和牛顿多面体的等价描述，揭示决策边界的几何结构如何随长度变化，从而解释泛化失败的结构性根源。

Method: 作者将每个有限min-plus DP表示为DAG最短路径，进一步等价为热带多项式，其扩展牛顿多面体编码决策边界。通过证明图、多项式、多面体三种表示为同构半环，利用半环的两个维度缩减操作（将变量设为两个单位元）分析映射的非单射性和非封闭性，并通过级联/并联组合及终端操作的表达能力刻画所有结构冗余。

Result: 证明了图、多项式、多面体三种DP描述在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面构成同构半环，并通过这些操作刻画了所有结构性冗余。提出两个结构性负结果：半环中两种天然的降维方式（将变量设为各恒等元）既非单射也非始终在DP内封闭；级联和并联组合无法从更小子DAG构造所有DAG拓扑，且即使所有终端操作也不足以捕获所有DP组合。

Conclusion: 长度T处的决策边界无法可靠地决定T+1处的决策边界，这一失败源于DP半环结构本身的内在限制而非简单的外推问题。两个结构性负结果表明，从几何和代数的角度看，改善DP长度泛化需要超越现有半环组合操作的框架。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins. We prove these three descriptions (graph, polynomial, polyhedron) form isomorphic semirings at two levels --- formal polynomials and their computed functions --- connected by operations that characterize all structural redundancies. We then address the length-generalization question geometrically: does the decision boundary at length $T$ decide the boundary at $T+1$? We present two structural negatives. The semiring's two native ways to reduce dimension (setting a variable to each identity) are neither injective nor always closed within the DP. Series and parallel composition fail to construct all DAG topologies from smaller sub-DAGs, and even all terminal-only operations do not capture all DP compositions.

</details>


### [242] [DeMMO: Longitudinal and Cross-Disease Modelling of Digital Mobility Outcomes via Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25073)
*Menghui Zhou,Zhipeng Yuan,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMMO是一个可解释的纵向、多疾病、多结局学习框架，用于从可穿戴传感器衍生的数字移动性结果（DMOs）中联合建模多种疾病中的多个临床结局，通过学习跨疾病和跨结局的符号关系实现选择性信息共享，无需配对参与者。在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集上，DeMMO显著优于九种强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMO研究通常一次只研究一种疾病、一次访视，无法建模多变量DMO关系与多个临床结局在疾病间如何联合演化。现有的时序多任务框架只能建模单一疾病内的进展，无法跨疾病联合建模多个预测结局，尤其是在疾病队列不共享参与者的情况下。

Method: DeMMO将每个疾病-结局目标表示为纵向DMO系数矩阵，结合时序正则化与稳定/访视特异性特征选择。核心技术创新是自动跨疾病和跨结局关系学习机制，直接从这些纵向映射中学习带符号的关系，实现无需配对参与者的选择性信息共享。

Result: 在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集（24个标准化真实世界DMO，5次访视，多种移动受限病症）上，DeMMO在总体和结局特异性预测性能上均取得最佳表现，对最强基线有显著提升。稳定性选择进一步识别出可靠的纵向DMO模式。

Conclusion: DeMMO填补了多疾病、多结局纵向DMO建模的空白，通过自动跨疾病关系学习实现了可解释且高性能的疾病进展监测，为后续临床验证和疾病监测提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Digital mobility outcomes (DMOs) derived from wearable sensors characterise mobility in daily life and offer a promising means of monitoring disease progression. Yet most DMO studies examine one disease at one visit; they do not model how multivariate DMO relationships with multiple clinical outcomes evolve jointly across diseases. Technically, existing temporal multi-task frameworks can model progression within an individual disease, but they do not jointly model multiple prediction outcomes across diseases, particularly when disease cohorts do not share participants. To address these gaps, we propose DeMMO, an interpretable framework for longitudinal, multi-disease, and multi-outcome learning. DeMMO represents each disease-outcome objective by a longitudinal DMO coefficient matrix and combines temporal regularisation with stable and visit-specific feature selection. Its central technical contribution is an automatic cross-disease and cross-outcome relation-learning mechanism that learns signed relations directly from these longitudinal mappings, enabling selective information sharing without paired participants. We evaluate DeMMO on the recently released, large-scale, multicentre Mobilise-D dataset, which provides a new opportunity to study 24 harmonised real-world DMOs over five visits across multiple mobility-limiting conditions. Against nine strong linear, longitudinal, and deep-regression baselines, DeMMO achieves the best overall and outcome-specific prediction performance, with significant improvements over the strongest baselines. Stability selection further identifies reliable longitudinal DMO patterns for subsequent clinical validation and disease monitoring. The implementation code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/menghui-zhou/DeMMO.

</details>


### [243] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过语义流量化信息在模型内部表示中的演化，构建滞后-预测范围属性矩阵，并借助结构保持扰动和稀疏局部代理模型生成可读且时间连贯的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在高风险场景中广泛使用，但其预测难以解释。现有事后解释方法往往忽略时间依赖性，且无法提供针对不同预测范围（horizon）的特定解释。

Method: 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流（semantic flow）量化信息在模型内部表示中的时间演化；通过聚合语义流构建滞后-预测范围属性矩阵；生成结构保持扰动并拟合稀疏局部代理模型，产出人类可读且时间连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上使用忠实度（faithfulness）和稳定性（stability）诊断评估。语义流变体的忠实度达到或优于标准事后基线，且计算效率显著更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出需要谨慎应用解释的场景。

Conclusion: 语义流方法为时间序列预测提供了高效且忠实的时间解析解释方案，同时通过稳定性诊断明确了其适用边界。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [244] [The Von-Neumann State-Space Transformer for neural decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25088)
*Morteza Sarafyazd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种冯·诺依曼启发的状态空间Transformer（VN-SST），其前馈模块通过低秩指令库合成每个token特有的算子，以低维状态空间记忆作为指令指针，在神经解码任务上显著提升数据效率和参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层计算本质上是低维的——少量潜变量调控高维神经响应。现有Transformer的前馈块对每个token应用相同算子，而传统的软专家混合仅混合输出而非算子本身，缺乏指令控制的灵活性。作者的目标是在有限数据和较小参数预算下实现高效的神经解码模型。

Method: 提出VN-SST（von-Neumann State-Space Transformer）：其前馈块由一个共享基础算子加一组可学习的低秩指令构成"低秩指令库"；每个token通过从携带的状态空间记忆的低维投影中读取一个代码，据此合成该token实际使用的权重矩阵。慢速的潜变量轨迹充当指令指针，模仿低维动态在皮层计算中的路由作用。

Result: 在三个运动皮层神经解码基准上，VN-SST比现代Transformer更数据高效：在数据最稀缺的基准上大幅领先，在其他两个上同样领先；长上下文下准确率不降反升。模型可将大量指令库压缩为每token数比特，证明程序容量作为控制通道而非精度杠杆。在两个小型语言建模基准上也更参数高效。

Conclusion: VN-SST验证了"控制器解码指令并执行token特有算子"这一冯·诺依曼式高效计算假设，表明将存储的程序（指令）与执行分离的架构不仅能提升神经解码的数据效率，还可能是一个通用机制，同时适用于语言建模等任务。

Abstract: Cortical computation is strikingly low-dimensional: a handful of latent variables, carried in a neural population's activity, steer the higher-dimensional responses of individual neurons. Our aim is sample efficiency-models that decode well from limited data and at small parameter budgets. In a standard Transformer layer, the feed-forward block applies the same operator to every token. We suggest a von-Neumann inspired hypothesis of efficient computation as an alternative for neural decoding: a controller decodes an instruction and then executes a token-specific operator; the usual realization-a soft mixture of experts-only blends their outputs, not operators. We introduce a von-Neumann State-Space Transformer (VN-SST), a memory-augmented Transformer whose feed-forward block is a low-rank instruction bank: a shared base operator plus a small set of learned low-rank instructions, from which a per-token code synthesizes the weight matrix actually used at that token. The code is read from a low- dimensional projection of a carried state-space memory, so a slow latent trajectory acts as an instruction pointer-mirroring how low-dimensional dynamics may route cortical computation. On three motor-cortex neural-decoding benchmarks, VN-SST is far more data-efficient than a modern Transformer, each jointly predicting spikes and decoding behavior. This model wins by a wide margin on the scarcest benchmark, leads on the other two, and turns longer context into rising rather than falling accuracy. We evaluated that the network compresses a large instruction bank to a few bits per token, so program capacity acts as a control channel, not an accuracy lever. The same model is also more parameter-efficient on two small text benchmarks used for language modeling (LLMs), suggesting a generic mechanism.

</details>


### [245] [Understanding the Energy Scaling of Large Language Model Inference Across Context Lengths and Attention Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25096)
*Molka Chkir,Syed Muhammad Danish,Jos Höll,Arghavan Asad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验研究了不同注意力架构（MHA、GQA、GQA+SWA）在解码阶段的GPU能耗特征，发现注意力机制是决定能耗随上下文长度增长的关键因素，其中MHA能耗增长最陡、GQA次之、GQA+SWA几乎恒定；模型规模决定绝对能耗，批处理可降低单个token能耗和延迟高达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）应用的日益普及，推理阶段的能耗和环境影响引起了广泛关注。然而，针对不同注意力架构在解码阶段能耗的系统性研究仍然缺乏，特别是注意力机制如何影响能耗随上下文长度变化的规律尚不明确，这阻碍了能源高效LLM架构的选择与推理配置的优化。

Method: 本文通过对四种代表性开源LLM（涵盖MHA、GQA、GQA+SWA三种注意力架构）进行系统性的实证研究，在不同上下文长度、批处理大小和生成负载下，使用NVIDIA硬件计数器测量GPU能耗。研究分析了上下文长度、注意力机制、KV缓存增长和批处理对解码阶段能耗的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：注意力机制是决定解码能耗随上下文长度缩放的主要因素。MHA模型的能耗随上下文增长曲线明显比GQA模型更陡，而带滑动窗口注意力（SWA）的GQA模型能耗几乎保持恒定。此外，模型规模主要决定绝对能耗水平，批处理可将每个生成token的能耗和请求延迟降低高达87%。

Conclusion: 该研究为选择能效友好的LLM架构和推理配置提供了实用指导：在长上下文推理场景中，GQA+SWA架构具有显著的能耗优势；通过合理配置批处理可以大幅提升推理能效并降低延迟。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models (LLMs) has raised increasing concerns about the energy consumption and environmental impact of inference. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of decode-phase energy consumption across representative open-source LLMs employing Multi-Head Attention (MHA), Grouped Query Attention (GQA), and Grouped Query Attention with Sliding Window Attention (SWA) to characterize how attention architecture influences decode-phase energy consumption under varying inference workloads. We evaluate four models across different context lengths, batch sizes, and generation workloads while measuring GPU energy using NVIDIA hardware counters. We examine the effects of context length, attention mechanism, Key-Value (KV) cache growth, and batching on decode-phase energy consumption. Results show that attention mechanism is the primary factor governing how decode energy scales with context length. MHA models exhibit substantially steeper energy growth than GQA models, whereas GQA with SWA maintains nearly constant energy consumption. We further show that model size primarily determines absolute energy consumption, while batching reduces both energy per generated token and request latency by up to 87%. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting energy-efficient LLM architectures and inference configurations.

</details>


### [246] [Flower Hub: A Reproducible Benchmarking Platform for Federated Learning in Simulation and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25114)
*Yan Gao,Mohammad Naseri,Javier Fernandez-Marques,Dimitris Stripelis,Lorenzo Sani,Davide Eynard,Fan Zhang,Hong Jia,Ting Dang,D. B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Ole Werger,Lars Wulfert,Petros Demetrakopoulos,Sofia Tsekeridou,InSeo Song,KangYoon Lee,Honghao Li,Lingjuan Lyu,John P Dickerson,Daniel Janes Beutel,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flower Hub平台，用于发布、发现和执行联邦学习应用，通过将基准测试打包为可执行的版本化应用，实现跨模拟与真实部署场景的可复现基准测试，并支持多领域任务和系统感知报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）的基准测试面临难以复现、难以比较和难以扩展的问题：现有评估通常绑定于定制基础设施、以不完整的研究代码发布，且主要在模拟中进行，缺乏可移植性和实际相关性。

Method: 提出Flower Hub平台，将基准测试封装为可执行、带版本管理的应用，具有标准化元数据、固定依赖和明确的评估工作流；构建覆盖跨孤岛（cross-silo）与跨设备（cross-device）设置的多域基准套件，涵盖医学影像、金融表格学习、法律指令微调、钓鱼URL检测和音频标注任务；同一基准应用可在模拟与部署运行时上运行而无需修改代码。

Result: 证明了Flower Hub能够实现可复现的联邦学习基准测试，且同一应用代码可跨模拟与部署环境统一运行；基准设计支持系统感知报告，包括运行时间和通信指标。

Conclusion: 该工作推动联邦学习基准测试从临时代码工件向可移植、可执行、可复用的基准应用转变。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a key approach for training models across decentralized data, yet benchmarking in FL remains difficult to reproduce, compare, and extend. Existing evaluations are often tied to custom infrastructure, released as incomplete research code, and conducted primarily in simulation, which limits portability and practical relevance. We present Flower Hub, a platform for publishing, discovering, and executing decentralized and federated applications. We show how it enables reproducible benchmarking by packaging benchmarks as executable, versioned applications with standardized metadata, pinned dependencies, and explicit evaluation workflows. We instantiate this approach with a multi-domain benchmark suite spanning cross-silo and cross-device settings, and including tasks in medical imaging, financial tabular learning, legal instruction tuning, phishing URL detection, and audio tagging. We further demonstrate that the same benchmarking application can run across both simulation and deployment runtimes without changing the application code, enabling unified evaluation across varying learning environments. Beyond model quality, our benchmark design supports system-aware reporting, including runtime and communication metrics. This work advances benchmarking in FL settings from ad hoc code artifacts towards portable, executable, and reusable benchmark applications.

</details>


### [247] [GRAPE: Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation for Query-Efficient High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25116)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAPE是一个两阶段的高维黑盒优化框架，通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验，并依据期望下降量选择更新方向，在对抗攻击和LLM提示优化任务上显著提升查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的局部贝叶斯优化方法在高维黑盒函数优化中常优先考虑下降概率而非下降幅度，导致步长过于保守、单次改进微小，浪费大量查询在几乎确定下降但收益甚微的方向上。

Method: GRAPE采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验（梯度精炼），第二阶段在选择更新方向时最大化条件于下降前提下的期望下降量（进度感知利用）。理论上证明了梯度精炼阶段能单调最小化局部不确定性，且随后的进度感知方向在后验锐化后收敛至真实最速下降方向。

Result: 在黑盒对抗攻击任务中，GRAPE相比基线实现了平均5.4倍的加速；在大型语言模型提示优化任务中，最终平均遗憾值比次优方法低3.8个对数单位。

Conclusion: GRAPE通过将梯度精炼与进度感知利用相结合，有效解决了局部贝叶斯优化中进度过小的问题，在高维黑盒优化任务中展现了卓越的查询效率和收敛能力。

Abstract: Optimizing expensive, high-dimensional black-box functions remains a central challenge in modern machine learning and scientific discovery. While local Bayesian optimization mitigates the curse of dimensionality, existing techniques often prioritize the probability of descent over the magnitude of progress. This leads to overly conservative steps that yield negligible improvement, wasting queries on directions that are nearly certain to descend but offer little decrease. We introduce Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation (GRAPE), a two-stage framework that first sharpens the local gradient posterior via a closed-form acquisition function, then selects update directions by maximizing the expected decrease conditional on descent. Theoretical analysis proves that this gradient refinement stage monotonically minimizes local uncertainty and that the progress-aware direction converges to true steepest descent as the posterior sharpens. Empirically, GRAPE demonstrates superior query efficiency across high-dimensional tasks: in black-box adversarial attacks, it achieves an average 5.4$\times$ speedup over baselines, and on large language model prompt optimization tasks, it outperforms the second best method by a reduction of 3.8 log-units in the final average regret.

</details>


### [248] [Toward Machine Learning with the Unit as a Primitive: Learning from Unit-Linked Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25118)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"单元"(unit)作为机器学习任务语义层面的显式原语——即持久性个体，数据样本所引用的对象。在监督学习中，学习目标被定义为单元条件响应律族，学习器学习的是(tokenizer, response-law)对。当身份未解析时，需要通过单元溯因(unit abduction)或可信解析器来形成单元令牌。形式化结果表明：无法链接的单行观测无法区分异构单元世界与同质池化世界，而可信的同单元配对可以分离受限见证集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习通过样本进行形式化，但样本所引用的持久性个体（单元）通常保持隐式。这种隐含性导致语义层面的缺失：样本条件分布无法判断世界是同质的还是异质的（即观测到的只是异构族的一个边缘分布）。作者主张将单元提升为任务语义层面的显式原语，以澄清学习任务的语义基础。

Method: 论文在任务语义层面引入单元(unit)作为显式原语，并定义相似性准则(population of persistent referents and sameness criterion)。监督学习被形式化为单元条件响应律族；学习器输出的是一对(T_φ, R_θ)：tokenizer生成上下文单元令牌，以及一个共享的响应律形式读取该令牌。结构化类将该形式视为令牌上的简单关系，线性预测器为运行实例。身份未解析时，引入单元溯因(unit abduction)从事实证据中形成同类型令牌，或由可信解析器提供查找令牌。

Result: 形式化结果给出了该监督专门化的关键区分能力：无法链接的单行观测（unlinked single-row observations）无法区分异构单元世界与同质池化世界——这是一个基本的不可辨识性结果；而可信的同单元配对（trusted same-unit pairs）能够分离出受限的见证集（restricted witness），从而在该受限范围内恢复辨识能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器学习的形式化基础引入了显式的单元原语，明确了同质性是世界侧的属性而非样本条件所能判定的事实，并建立了身份未解析时的学习机制（tokenizer + shared law + abduction）。这为处理身份模糊、跨行引用同一实体的学习任务提供了语义清晰的形式框架，并通过可辨识性结果刻画了数据能够与不能判定的范围。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually formalized through samples, while the persistent individual to which multiple observed or possible events refer often remains implicit. We propose the \emph{unit} as an explicit primitive at the level of task semantics. A learning task first declares a population of persistent referents and a sameness criterion; the realized value $u$ denotes the selected referent. Supervised learning is the main formal specialization. Its semantic object is a family of unit-conditioned response laws. Homogeneity is the special case in which those laws coincide; a sample-only conditional is silent as to whether the world is homogeneous or the observed law is only the marginal of a heterogeneous family. What is learned from data is a pair $(T_φ,R_θ)$: a tokenizer that produces a contextual unit token and one shared response-law form that reads it. The structured class takes that form to be a simple relation in the token; a linear predictor is the running instance. The token is the learner-side representation through which the task-side unit affects prediction, while a learner specification that omits unit information is unit-insensitive; homogeneity remains a property of the world-side response family. When identity is unresolved, the world-side law mixes unit-conditioned targets, while the learner composes its shared form with a token. A trusted resolver may fix the unit and supply a lookup token; otherwise \emph{unit abduction} forms a token of the same type from factual evidence. Unlinked single-row observations can fail to distinguish a heterogeneous unit world from a homogeneous pooled world; trusted same-unit pairs separate a restricted witness. The formal results concern this supervised specialization.

</details>


### [249] [Multimodal Injury Risk Prediction in Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25126)
*Francisco Erramuspe Alvarez,Shobharani Polasa,Weihao Qu,Jay Wang,Ling Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态预测运动员状态框架PART（Predictive Athlete Readiness framework for Tennis），通过整合九名大学网球运动员的多种数据源（生理指标、训练和比赛数据、睡眠数据、自我报告问卷、跳跃评估和比赛视频动作分析），利用机器学习和深度学习技术评估网球运动员的表现和受伤风险。PART捕捉运动员的整体健康状态、受伤风险、身体能力和打球风格四个特征，并通过监督学习整合这些特征，提供整体状态评估及特定身体部位（如上肢肘部、下肢膝盖）的风险预测，在预测效果上表现良好且对业余网球运动员也有应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动表现和受伤风险预测研究大多依赖主观观察和专家评估，限制了其有效性。虽然足球、篮球、摔跤等领域已尝试整合可穿戴设备等多源数据来提升准确性，但网球领域的类似研究仍几乎空白。作者希望填补这一空白，提出一个更客观、全面的网球运动员状态评估框架。

Method: PART框架采用多模态方法，收集来自九名大学网球运动员的六类数据（生理指标、训练和比赛数据、可穿戴设备睡眠数据、每日问卷自我报告、跳跃评估、比赛视频动作分析），通过机器学习和深度学习技术处理这些数据，提取网球运动员的四个关键特征维度：整体健康状态（overall wellness）、受伤风险（injury risk）、身体能力（physical capability）和打球风格（playing style），最后通过监督学习整合这四个特征，输出整体状态评估和身体特定部位的风险预测。

Result: 基于九名大学网球运动员数据的评估表明，PART在预测整体健康状态和受伤风险方面取得了良好的性能表现。此外，该框架对业余网球爱好者也显示出应用潜力，因为业余选手常因不正确的击球技术而受伤。

Conclusion: PART是首个针对网球领域的多模态运动员状态预测框架，克服了传统主观评估的局限，能够提供更客观、更全面的网球运动员健康和受伤风险评估，为职业和业余网球选手的训练决策和伤病预防提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Machine learning has had a significant positive impact on the prediction of athlete performance and injury risk. Most works in this field rely on subjective observations and expert assessments, which restrict their effectiveness. In sports like soccer, basketball, and wrestling, some studies attempt to address this challenge by integrating data from alternative sources, such as readings from wearable devices, alongside traditional subjective observations and expert assessments to enhance accuracy. However, similar research in tennis remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we propose a multimodal Predictive Athlete Readiness framework for Tennis (PART) to assess both performance and injury risk in tennis players. By leveraging machine learning and deep learning techniques, PART processes multiple sources of data collected from nine collegiate tennis players, including physiological metrics, training and match data, sleep data from wearable devices, self-reported information via daily questionnaires, jump assessments, and motion analysis from match play videos. PART captures four characteristics of tennis players: overall wellness, injury risk, physical capability, and playing style. By integrating these four characteristics by supervised learning, it is capable of providing a holistic assessment of the tennis athlete's condition, along with advanced forecasts of specific body areas at risk such as the upper body (e.g., elbows) or lower body (e.g., knees). Our evaluation, conducted with data from nine collegiate tennis players, shows that PART achieves strong performance in predicting both overall wellness and injury risk. Additionally, our framework also shows promise for recreational tennis players, who often suffer from injuries due to incorrect playing techniques.

</details>


### [250] [When Does Context Routing Help? A Systematic Study of Multi-Modal Fusion in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25128)
*Ruizhe Zhou,Gaoyuan Du,Xiaoyang Liu,Haoqi Yao,Deepayan Chakrabarti,Jiating Lin,Yixuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究多模态时间序列预测中辅助上下文何时能真正带来收益的问题，提出两个数据集层面的必要条件（目标序列不被last-value捷径主导——低自相关ρ_h；上下文携带超出历史之外的条件互信息δ>0），并通过受控实验和因果干预验证了这两个条件的充分性：当两个条件同时满足时，文本条件专家调制带来显著的MSE降低；任一条件失败时，贡献坍缩到容量下限且无可归因于上下文的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态时间序列预测方法声称通过日益复杂的融合机制将辅助上下文融入时序预测并报告显著收益，但这些收益是否真正来源于对上下文的利用，还是仅仅源于架构的附带效应，往往难以判断。作者希望提出一个可检验的、更窄的问题：辅助上下文何时能为预测器带来帮助。

Method: 作者识别出两个必须同时满足的数据集级条件：(1) 目标序列不被last-value捷径主导（低自相关ρ_h）；(2) 上下文携带超出历史的条件互信息δ>0（δ=0时无预测器可获益，为分布无关结果）。通过MoME（14.3B参数的混合专家模型）在6个数据集10个种子上的受控实验，以及单一骨干测试床中4种额外融合机制的验证，并设计两个因果干预实验：为主干添加捷径抑制MoME路由贡献77-93%（3个数据集）；逐步破坏上下文质量使上下文特定收益从+44%变为负值。同时在27个Monash Archive数据集上验证自相关组分，并提供校准的预训练诊断。

Result: 当两个条件都成立时，文本条件专家调制带来显著的MSE降低；当任一条件失败时，贡献坍缩到调制通路的容量下限，且不携带可归因于上下文的信号。因果干预实验证实了该论断。诊断在测试数据集上无假阳性。作者明确指出了证据的不对称性：阴性结果是广泛可靠的，而大幅正向收益仅来自单一模型族（MoME），测试床仅在方向上予以佐证。

Conclusion: 辅助上下文能否帮助预测器，取决于两个可检验的数据集级条件：低自相关（目标不被捷径主导）和正条件互信息（上下文携带超出历史的信号）。该诊断可作为预训练前的校准工具，帮助研究者判断融合架构是否可能从上下文中获益。但正向结论的外推需谨慎，因其主要证据来自单一模型族。

Abstract: Multi-modal time series forecasting methods integrate auxiliary context into temporal predictions through increasingly sophisticated fusion mechanisms. A growing body of work reports substantial gains, yet it is often unclear whether they reflect genuine use of the context or incidental architectural effects. We ask a narrower, checkable question: when can auxiliary context help a forecaster at all?
  We identify two dataset-level conditions that must both hold: (1) the target is not dominated by a last-value shortcut (low autocorrelation rho_h), and (2) the context carries information about the target beyond history (non-zero conditional mutual information delta; when delta=0 no predictor can benefit---a distribution-free result). Through controlled experiments on MoME (a 14.3B-parameter mixture-of-experts model, 6 datasets, 10 seeds) and four additional fusion mechanisms implemented within a single-backbone testbed (5 datasets), we find that when both conditions hold, text-conditioned expert modulation contributes a sizeable MSE reduction; when either fails, the contribution collapses to the capacity floor of the modulation pathway and carries no context-attributable signal.
  We establish causality through two interventions: adding a shortcut to MoME suppresses routing contribution by 77-93% across 3 datasets; progressively corrupting context quality drives the context-specific benefit from +44% to negative. We validate the autocorrelation component of our diagnostic on 27 Monash Archive datasets. We provide a calibrated pre-training diagnostic that, on the datasets we test, yields no false positives in well-powered settings. We are explicit about the asymmetry of our evidence: the negative arm is broadly reliable, while the large positive magnitudes come from a single model family (MoME) and are corroborated only in direction by the testbed.

</details>


### [251] [Rethinking the Transferable Adversarial Attacks and Robust Defense in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25133)
*Zuobin Xiong,Deval Mukherjee,Homook Cho,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析联邦学习中对抗样本在不同客户端模型间的可迁移性，并基于对抗训练的鲁棒性迁移设计防御机制，以缓解可迁移对抗样本攻击对联邦学习系统的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能保护用户隐私，但大量用户参与引入了投毒攻击、拜占庭攻击和对抗样本攻击等安全威胁。已有投毒和拜占庭攻击在真实联邦学习场景下因强假设（如客户端选择率、恶意攻击者比例）而渗透率不佳，且对抗样本攻击与客户端数据分布之间的关系尚不明确，需要深入分析。

Method: 1) 分析对抗样本在不同客户端模型之间的可迁移性，建立对抗样本与客户端数据分布之间的关联；2) 基于模型鲁棒性的可迁移性设计对抗训练驱动的防御机制；3) 通过理论分析可迁移性来理解联邦学习系统的脆弱性。

Result: 在真实数据集上多种设置下的实验评估表明，所提出的对抗攻击与防御方法在性能上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过对抗样本可迁移性的理论分析，揭示了联邦学习系统中对抗样本与脆弱性的内在关系，所提出的攻击与防御框架在真实场景下具有有效性和优越性。

Abstract: The development of federated learning (FL) techniques has helped improve the privacy preservation of users' data and extended the applications of machine learning models. However, the involvement of a large number of users in FL also creates open opportunities for different adversaries, such as poisoning attacks, Byzantine attacks, and adversarial example attacks. Yet, recent research has disclosed that existing poisoning attacks and Byzantine attacks can not achieve satisfactory penetration in realistic FL scenarios caused by strong assumptions, \textit{e.g.,} client selection rate, and the ratio of malicious attackers. In this paper, the transferability of adversarial examples among different client models is analyzed to understand the relation between adversarial examples and clients' data distribution. Moreover, to mitigate the attacks of transferable adversarial examples, we design a defense mechanism stemming from the transferability of model robustness by adversarial training. As a result, through theoretical analysis of transferability, we gain insights into adversarial examples and the vulnerability of federated learning systems. Our proposed adversarial attack and defense methods are evaluated via real-life datasets in various settings to show their performance over the existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [252] [Drift Variation Autoencoder: Unifying Generation and Representation Learning through Conditional Posterior Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25138)
*Jiarui Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出漂移变分自编码器（Drift Variation autoencoder），将条件后验P(X|C)作为条件生成和生成充分表示学习的统一统计对象。通过掩码编码器加条件流解码器，在理论上证明了基于Flow Matching的干净预测风险与轮廓条件KL具有相同的后验充分零集，并在CrossGeom-4基准上验证了可观测因子线性探针R²约0.999、条件误差提升13.5-15.7倍、联合目标注意力使未观测因子分歧降低90%以上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机掩码、裁剪或模态移除使确定性重建成为不完整的目标：一个观测可以对应多种干净补全。需要将对应的后验分布作为条件生成和生成充分表示学习的共同统计对象，而不是追求单一确定性重建。

Method: 提出Drift Variation自编码器，包含掩码编码器Z=E(C)和条件流解码器，使用干净预测的Flow Matching损失。理论上将理想条件KL分解为生成器近似和表示缺陷I(X;C|Z)两部分，并为条件Flow Matching推导正交风险分解。

Result: 在CrossGeom-4的18次受控基准实验中，可观测因子线性探针R²达0.9990-0.9992；打乱联合模型的编码器条件使条件误差增加13.5-15.7倍；联合目标注意力相比独立目标解码器将共享未观测因子的分歧降低90.1-92.8%。可观测模态也被生成和重建以验证完整元组目标。

Conclusion: 在仿射高斯路径下，干净预测表示间隙为零当且仅当P(X|Z)=P(X|C)，因此Flow Matching引起的编码器相关超额干净预测风险与轮廓理想条件KL具有相同的后验充分零集。联合理想最优处，零噪声端点的精确条件场能生成P(X|Z)进而P(X|C)。该结果可扩展到连续多模态积空间。但无条件模式平衡仍不完美，实证主张限于受控多模态概念验证。

Abstract: Stochastic masking, cropping, or modality removal makes deterministic reconstruction an incomplete target: one observation can admit many clean completions. This work takes the corresponding posterior $P(X\mid C)$ as the common statistical object for conditional generation and generatively sufficient representation learning. Drift Variation autoencoder trains a masked encoder $Z=E(C)$ and a conditional flow decoder with one clean-prediction Flow Matching loss. The analysis first decomposes the ideal conditional KL into generator approximation and the representation deficiency $I(X;C\mid Z)$. It then derives orthogonal risk decompositions for conditional Flow Matching. For an affine Gaussian path, the clean-prediction representation gap is zero if and only if $P(X\mid Z)=P(X\mid C)$. Thus the encoder-dependent excess clean-prediction risk induced by Flow Matching and the profiled ideal conditional KL have the same posterior-sufficient zero set, without being numerically equal objectives. An exact conditional field with a zero-noise endpoint then generates $P(X\mid Z)$ and hence $P(X\mid C)$ at a joint ideal optimum. The result extends to continuous multimodal product spaces when the complete modality tuple remains the Flow target for every observation mask. On CrossGeom-4, an 18-run controlled benchmark, observable factors have linear-probe $R^2$ of $0.9990$-$0.9992$, shuffling the joint model's encoder condition increases conditional error by $13.5\times$-$15.7\times$, and joint target attention reduces disagreement on an unobserved factor shared by two outputs by $90.1$-$92.8\%$ relative to independent target decoders. Visible modalities are also generated and reconstructed, directly validating the full-tuple objective. Unconditional mode balance remains imperfect, delimiting the empirical claim to a controlled multimodal proof of concept.

</details>


### [253] [SNAP-KG: Streaming Node Assignment via Projection for Knowledge Graph Entity Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25149)
*Jui-Chien Lin,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SNAP-KG框架，用于知识图谱流式节点分配与归纳推理。通过投影器将新到达实体的原始特征直接映射到嵌入空间，无需重训练即可完成聚类分配，在五个基准数据集和240万节点的生产级KG上验证了数数量级的推理加速和竞争力的聚类质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱构建流水线必须持续将新到达的实体整合进不断增长的图中。新实体在获取阶段仅有原始特征向量、缺乏图连通性，需先分配到语义社区才能进行实体解析和链接预测。现有基于多视图图聚类的方法虽是转导式的，假设图固定，无法为未见实体分配类别，必须重训练。因此需要一个支持流式实体归纳推理的框架。

Method: SNAP-KG训练一个投影器，仅使用原始特征即将新实体直接映射到学习到的嵌入空间，从而无需图访问或模型重训练即可进行即时聚类分配。该方法支持图结构多视图关系聚类与归纳推理，服务于流式实体整合场景。

Result: 在五个基准多视图图数据集和240万节点的生产级KG上，相比重训练方法实现了多个数量级的推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的聚类质量。作为下游任务的候选范围缩小机制，在五个基准数据集上实现了62-75%的候选搜索缩减，在OGB-WikiKG2上实现了97%的缩减（用于实体解析和链接预测）。

Conclusion: SNAP-KG是一种有效的流式知识图谱实体整合框架，通过投影学习实现归纳式聚类分配，在推理效率上大幅优于重训练方案，同时显著缩小下游实体解析和链接预测任务的候选搜索空间，具备生产级可扩展性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) construction pipelines must continuously integrate newly arriving entities into a growing graph. Unlike inserting triples between existing nodes, a newly arriving entity has no graph connectivity: it emerges from the acquisition phase as a raw feature vector and must be assigned to a semantic community before entity resolution and link prediction can operate over a tractable candidate set. Existing multi-view graph clustering methods exploit multiple relation types as structural views, but are transductive: they assume a fixed graph and cannot assign unseen entities without retraining. We propose SNAP-KG (Streaming Node Assignment via Projection for Knowledge Graph Entity Integration), a framework supporting graph-structural multi-view relational clustering and inductive inference for streaming entities. SNAP-KG trains a projector to map a new entity directly to the learned embedding space using only raw features, enabling immediate cluster assignment without graph access or model retraining. Experiments on five benchmark multi-view graph datasets and a production-scale KG of 2.4 million nodes demonstrate multiple orders-of-magnitude inference speedups over retraining-based approaches and competitive clustering quality. As a candidate scoping mechanism for downstream tasks, SNAP-KG achieves 62-75% candidate search reduction on the five benchmark datasets and 97% on OGB-WikiKG2 for entity resolution and link prediction.

</details>


### [254] [Bayesian Flow Networks for Offline Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25163)
*Ludvig Killingberg,Helge Langseth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BFN-RL，一个基于贝叶斯流网络（BFNs）的统一生成建模框架，用于离线强化学习。该框架通过迭代演化分布参数而非噪声数据实例，原生支持离散和连续轨迹空间的建模，并在离散规划和连续控制任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习依赖静态数据集学习决策策略，无需实时环境交互。现有基于序列建模的方法主要使用连续扩散模型进行轨迹合成，但当应用于离散规划任务时，需要类别化构建而非标准高斯构建，这限制了这些方法在离散和连续轨迹空间中的统一应用。因此需要一个能够同时处理离散与连续模态的统一生成框架。

Method: BFN-RL基于贝叶斯流网络（BFNs）构建统一生成模型。其核心思想是迭代演化分布参数而非噪声数据实例，从而在单一概率框架内原生建模离散和连续轨迹空间。具体包括：(1) 类别化规划器（categorical planner）用于生成未来状态序列；(2) 学习得到的逆动力学模型（inverse-dynamics model）将连续生成的状态转换为动作。

Result: 在离散规划和连续控制任务上的评估表明，BFN-RL能够在类别化状态空间与连续状态空间中均生成有效的轨迹，展示了该框架跨数据模态的生成能力。

Conclusion: BFN-RL证明了贝叶斯流网络可以作为离线轨迹规划的通用生成基础，在不同数据模态（离散与连续）上均表现出良好的性能，为离线强化学习中的轨迹生成提供了统一的概率建模方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) leverages static datasets to learn decision policies without real-time environment interaction. While recent sequence-modeling approaches rely on continuous diffusion models for trajectory synthesis, applying these methods to discrete planning tasks requires a categorical formulation rather than the standard Gaussian construction. We present BFN-RL, a unified generative modeling framework for offline RL based on Bayesian Flow Networks (BFNs). By iteratively evolving distribution parameters rather than noisy data instances, BFN-RL natively models both discrete and continuous trajectory spaces within a single probabilistic formulation. The categorical planner generates future state sequences, and a learned inverse-dynamics model converts consecutive generated states into actions. Evaluations in discrete planning and continuous control show that BFN-RL can generate effective trajectories across both categorical and continuous state spaces. Our results establish BFNs as a versatile generative foundation for offline trajectory planning across data modalities.

</details>


### [255] [Simultaneous inference of environmental and interaction forces in collective dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25181)
*Nipuni de Silva,Ming Zhong,James M. Greene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个扩展的变分学习框架,用于从轨迹数据中同时非参数地推断集体动力学系统中的交互核,并通过半参数或完全非参数表示学习环境力,还引入了基于非参数学习的模型选择方法来识别最优解释观测数据的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体动力学系统(如细胞迁移、群体机器人、社会动力学)的核心问题是理解局部交互如何产生大规模协调行为。现有方程学习方法可能忽略底层物理(集体动力学),而本文旨在开发一种通用的非参数学习框架,无需对交互核的分析形式做先验假设,同时能够处理交互核和环境/个体内部力。

Method: 扩展现有的变分学习方法,使其适用于同时包含交互核和环境/个体内部力的集体系统。框架同时执行:1)非参数地推断交互核;2)通过半参数或完全非参数表示学习环境力。此外,利用非参数学习框架的特征识别能力,引入模型选择程序来区分不同的集体动力学框架并恢复机制性交互机制。

Result: 该方法在多个基准模型上得到验证,涵盖同步、对齐、吸引-排斥以及外部环境力等集体动力学现象。模型选择程序能够从轨迹数据中有效区分不同的集体动力学框架。

Conclusion: 基于核函数的非参数学习框架通过融入集体动力学的底层物理,比通用的方程学习方法具有更好的模型准确性和预测能力;所提出的模型选择程序可直接从轨迹数据中恢复交互机制,为理解和识别集体动力学的底层交互规律提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Collective dynamics arise in a wide range of physical, biological, and engineering applications. Examples include cell migration, swarm robotics, social dynamics, and animal behavior. A defining characteristic of these systems is the emergence of large-scale coordination from local interactions among agents; a fundamental question is thus to understand the local interactions that give rise to the observed emergent dynamics. We are interested in methods for learning interactions generally, which can describe a wide class of physical systems exhibiting collective dynamics defined by an interaction kernel, without a priori assumptions on the analytical form of this kernel (i.e. it is nonparametric). The advantage of this kernel-based approach is that it incorporates the underlying physics of the model (i.e. collective dynamics), which more general equation-learning approaches may ignore, potentially limiting their effectiveness for model accuracy and predictions. In this work, we extend existing variational learning approaches to collective systems with both interaction kernels and environmental/intra-agent forces. The proposed framework simultaneously infers the interaction kernel non-parametrically while learning the environmental force using either semi-parametric or fully nonparametric representations. The methodology is validated on several benchmark models exhibiting synchronization, alignment, attraction-repulsion, and external environmental forces. We also introduce a model-selection procedure based on our nonparametric learning framework to identify models that optimally explain a given set of trajectory observations. By exploiting the feature-identification capability of the learned models, the proposed procedure can distinguish among different collective dynamics frameworks and recover mechanistic interaction mechanisms directly from trajectory data.

</details>


### [256] [What Should a Large Language Model See? Physical Invariants as a Data Representation for PDE Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25189)
*Fan Yang,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据解读"（data interpretation）方法，通过将时空场数据转化为理论家会参考的量（而非原始数据或仅通过评分函数间接学习），使大语言模型能够直接读取并用于自动构建场论。在模拟场基准测试中，该方法将恢复方程的准确率相比直接展示原始数据提高了近三倍，且无需训练、计算成本可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中，分子相互作用决定宏观行为的理解是核心挑战，但传统理论构建无法跟上现代实验产生的大规模数据。大语言模型为自动化理论构建提供了有前景的路径，但时空场无法直接放入提示（prompt）中，现有模型仅通过拟合优度评分的间接方式学习数据，信息利用效率低下。

Method: 提出"数据解读"（data interpretation）阶段：将场（field）测量为理论家会查阅的量化指标（如梯度、散度、拉普拉斯算符等物理量），将这些量作为直接输入提供给大语言模型，而非直接展示原始时空场数据。该方法无需任何训练，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在模拟场的基准测试上，相比直接展示原始数据，数据解读方法将恢复方程的准确率几乎提高了三倍，且几乎不增加计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过让语言模型像理论家一样阅读场数据，数据解读为自动化场论构建提供了一条实用路径，能够与实验过程协同演进。

Abstract: Understanding how molecular interactions govern macroscopic behaviour is a central challenge in molecular sciences. However, conventional theory building cannot keep pace with the vast datasets modern experimentation routinely produces. Large language models offer a promising route to automating theory construction, but a spatiotemporal field cannot be directly placed in a prompt. Existing models generally learn about the data only through a score measuring how well each proposal fits it. Here we introduce data interpretation, a stage that measures the field into the quantities a theorist would consult and supplies them to the model as a direct input. On a benchmark of simulated fields, interpretation nearly triples the accuracy of recovered equations relative to showing the raw data, at negligible computational cost and without any training. By allowing a language model to read field data as a theorist does, data interpretation offers a practical route to automated field theory construction that can coevolve with experimentation.

</details>


### [257] [Hyperbolic Latent Geometry for Tree-Structured Prototype Networks: A Local-vs-Global Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25199)
*Peter Flo,Luca Grossmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在层次分类模型中，类别原型所在潜在流形的选择（欧几里得空间R^d vs 庞加莱球B^d_c）如何影响树结构正则化器被满足的程度而不扭曲数据似然。通过在WikiArt数据集（27个风格类，81,446幅画作，使用冻结的CLIP ViT-B/16特征）上进行150次种子复制的正则化最大似然拟合实验，发现庞加莱原型在保持潜在空间中最近邻图的拓扑结构方面显著优于欧几里得原型（兄弟节点召回@5 +8.7个百分点，表亲召回 +15.2个百分点，配对t检验p < 10^-4），该差距在三种参考树定义下均成立。然而在分类性能上，欧几里得原型与在原始编码器特征上的逻辑回归持平，表明潜在几何没有可检测的贡献；只有双曲拟合在局部检索方面优于k-NN编码器基线。全局树保真度比较在不同参考树间不稳定，作者未声称全局优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次分类模型中常采用树结构正则化器来约束类别原型布局，但原型所在的潜在流形选择（欧几里得或双曲空间）是否影响正则化器的可满足性以及数据似然是否被扭曲，此前缺乏实证研究。由于双曲空间具有指数体积增长，理论上应能以更低失真嵌入树结构，本文旨在通过大规模实证来验证这一理论预期。

Method: 在层次分类模型中对类别原型分别采用欧几里得空间R^d和庞加莱球B^d_c两种潜在流形，施加相同的树结构正则化器，并通过正则化最大似然估计进行拟合。实验覆盖嵌入维度、曲率和正则化强度三个因素，总共进行150次种子复制的拟合。数据集为WikiArt（27个风格，81,446幅画作，使用冻结的CLIP ViT-B/16特征）。评估包括：最近邻图的拓扑保持（兄弟/表亲节点召回率）、分类性能（与逻辑回归和k-NN基线对比）以及全局树保真度（使用三种参考树定义：手工构建谱系、CLIP派生树和DINOv2派生树）。

Result: 发现了一个稳健的核心效应：庞加莱原型在潜在空间中保持最近邻图拓扑的能力显著优于欧几里得原型（兄弟召回@5 +8.7个百分点，表亲召回 +15.2个百分点，配对t检验p < 10^-4，符号一致性0.94），且该差距在三种参考树定义下均存在。分类方面，欧几里得原型与原始编码器特征上的逻辑回归性能持平，表明潜在几何无可检测的分类贡献；仅双曲拟合在局部检索上优于k-NN编码器基线。全局树保真度比较在不同参考树间不稳定，未声明全局赢家。

Conclusion: 在真实层次分类问题上，双曲几何（庞加莱球）与欧几里得几何作为类结构正则化器的潜在空间，表现出实证分离：双曲空间在局部拓扑保持（最近邻图保真度）上明确优于欧几里得空间，但在分类准确率上无额外收益，全局树保真度结论不确定。这一结果既验证了双曲空间嵌入树结构的理论优势在局部层面的现实体现，也提示潜在几何的选择对全局树结构和判别性能的影响可能并非一维的优劣关系。

Abstract: We study a tree-structured regularizer over class-prototype layouts in a hierarchical-classification model and ask whether the choice of latent manifold for the prototypes (Euclidean R^d vs. the Poincare ball B^d_c) affects how well that regularizer can be satisfied without distorting the data likelihood. The two manifolds differ only in their volume growth: hyperbolic space grows exponentially with radius and embeds trees with provably lower distortion than R^d of matched dimension, so the structured regularizer should be cheaper to satisfy on B^d_c. Across 150 seed-replicated regularized maximum-likelihood fits spanning embedding dimension, curvature, and regularizer strength on WikiArt (27 styles, 81,446 paintings, frozen CLIP ViT-B/16 features), we find a single robust effect: Poincare prototypes preserve the topology of the nearest-neighbor graph in latent space substantially better than matched Euclidean prototypes (sibling recall@5 +8.7 pp, cousin recall +15.2 pp; paired-t p < 10^-4, sign agreement 0.94), and the gap holds across three reference-tree definitions (hand-built lineage, CLIP-derived, and DINOv2-derived). On classification, Euclidean prototypes are tied with logistic regression on raw encoder features, indicating no detectable contribution from the latent geometry; only the hyperbolic fit improves on a k-NN encoder baseline for local retrieval. Global tree-fidelity comparisons are unstable across reference trees and we do not claim a winner. The results give an empirical separation, on a real hierarchical-classification problem, between two natural latent geometries for a class-structured regularizer.

</details>


### [258] [Learning Mixtures of Plackett-Luce Models for Multi-Objective Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25200)
*Dongyue Li,Ziniu Zhang,Lu Wang,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoPLEx，一种用于学习k个Plackett-Luce混合模型的算法，通过先用基础语言模型扩充排序数据再用梯度估计降低推理成本，最后用EM算法拟合混合模型，在偏好优化数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合Bradley-Terry模型仅处理成对比较，且当k超过m/2时混合模型理论上不可识别。需解决多路排序数据中异质偏好的混合建模问题，以实现更好的AI对齐和偏好优化。

Method: MoPLEx包含两个核心步骤：(1) 使用基础语言模型生成新响应来扩充排序数据至更大规模，解决不可识别性问题；(2) 采用基于梯度的估计在输入嵌入空间中降低推理成本。基于这两步设计期望最大化(EM)算法来拟合Plackett-Luce混合模型。

Result: 在多达340亿参数的模型上，梯度近似方法估计真实概率的误差小于5%；在偏好优化数据集上，MoPLEx相比单排序和混合Bradley-Terry模型基线，聚类准确率平均提升43.7%，排序准确率平均提升15.2%。

Conclusion: MoPLEx通过数据增强和梯度测量有效解决了多路排序中异质偏好混合建模的不可识别性问题，实验证明其在聚类和排序准确率上均显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning a mixture of $k$ Plackett-Luce models given multi-way ranking responses from annotators that may represent heterogeneous underlying preferences. This problem has many applications in AI alignment and preference optimization. Prior work has studied mixtures of Bradley-Terry models from pairwise comparisons. However, uncovering mixture models is theoretically unidentifiable when $k$ exceeds $m/2$, where $m$ is the length of a ranking. We propose an efficient implementation to address this limitation, which involves first augmenting the rankings to a larger size by generating new responses from a base language model, followed by a gradient-based estimation to reduce inference cost in the input embedding space. Based on this procedure, we then design an expectation-maximization algorithm with these two steps to fit a mixture of Plackett-Luce models, called MoPLEx. Extensive experiments are conducted to verify this approach. First, we show that the gradient-based approximation estimates true probabilities with less than 5% error on models with up to 34 billion parameters. Second, we show that MoPLEx improves clustering and ranking accuracy by an average of 43.7% and 15.2% over baselines using single ranking and mixtures of Bradley-Terry models, on preference optimization datasets. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of MoPLEx for tackling multi-way rankings from heterogeneous preferences through measuring alignment between gradients.

</details>


### [259] [LibriBrain100: One Hundred Hours of Broad and Deep MEG Data for Neural Speech Decoding at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25204)
*Francesco Mantegna,Dulhan Jayalath,Gereon Elvers,Tasha Kim,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,SungJun Cho,Teyun Kwon,Luisa Kurth,Miran Özdogan,Gilad Landau,Pratik Somaiya,Natalie Voets,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍LibriBrain100，一个大规模MEG语音解码数据集，包含超过100小时的高质量脑磁图数据（其中单被试约80小时），并配以标准化的评估协议、开源Python库和公开竞赛排行榜，推动非侵入性脑机接口语音解码研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEG语音解码数据集规模有限，缺少标准化评估框架，难以支撑可复现的比较研究；同时单被试数据深度和多被试覆盖广度之间的权衡尚不明确，阻碍了迈向实际非侵入性脑机接口应用的进展。

Method: (1) 构建LibriBrain100数据集，包含100+小时MEG自然连续语音记录，其中单被试约80小时（深度优先设计），另收集32名被试各约40分钟的跨被试数据；(2) 在词分类基准任务上，使用现有解码模型评估单被试大规模数据的性能；(3) 通过预训练模型的监督微调，展示跨被试数据对有限单被试数据的补偿作用；(4) 提供标准训练/验证/测试划分，开源Python库支持数据下载、预处理和深度学习框架的数据加载；(5) 发布公开机器学习竞赛与排行榜用于标准化基准评测。

Result: 在词分类基准上取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能，验证了记录质量和深度优先的单一被试数据规模的价值；同时证明跨被试的监督微调可显著弥补单被试数据量不足的问题。

Conclusion: LibriBrain100通过深度与广度兼顾的数据设计和标准化评测基础设施，为MEG语音解码提供了更可靠的基准，有望加速非侵入性脑机接口在严重瘫痪患者沟通恢复方面的实际应用。

Abstract: We introduce LibriBrain100, a large-scale MEG dataset for speech decoding designed from the ground up for reproducible, standardised evaluation. LibriBrain100 more than doubles the size of the original LibriBrain release, resulting in over 100 hours of high-quality MEG acquired while subjects listened to naturalistic continuous speech. With $\sim$80 hours from a single subject, LibriBrain100 sets a new record for deep, within-subject neural data (8$\times$ more than the next comparable dataset and roughly 80$\times$ more than other datasets). To demonstrate the payoff of this depth-first design, we evaluate on a word-classification benchmark---an increasingly well-established stepping stone towards the open challenge of noninvasive brain-to-text decoding. Using an existing decoding model, we achieve state-of-the-art performance---validating both the quality of the recordings and the value of within-subject data at scale. Because collecting 80 hours of data per user is impractical for real-world applications, we also collected $\sim$40 minutes of additional data from each of 32 subjects. Using the same word-classification benchmark, we demonstrate the value of broad multi-subject data: supervised finetuning of a pre-trained model can substantially compensate for limited per-subject data. We provide standard train, validation, and test splits, all reproducible through an open-sourced Python library that supports easy downloading, optional preprocessing, and data loading for common deep learning frameworks. In addition, the dataset and evaluation infrastructure are being released alongside an open machine-learning competition with a public leaderboard for standardised benchmarking. Ultimately, our hope is that LibriBrain100 will accelerate progress towards practical non-invasive brain-computer interfaces, capable of restoring communication to people living with severe paralysis.

</details>


### [260] [Representing MAX functions using two-hidden-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25221)
*Zhimao Wang,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用计算机辅助搜索，为MAX_5至MAX_8函数找到了两层隐藏层ReLU神经网络的精确表示，通过构造特定形式的有限线性组合来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定用两层隐藏层ReLU神经网络精确表示MAX_N函数所需的最少隐藏层数是一个长期悬而未决的问题。已有下界为2，上界为对数级别，但能否用常数层（甚至恰好2层）表示仍完全开放。此前MAX_5已被解决，但N≥6的情况被列为开放问题，本文旨在通过系统搜索填补这一空白。

Method: 采用计算机辅助搜索方法，考虑形如max{∑max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),∑max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})}项的有理线性组合。每个内层二元最大值可在第一隐藏层计算，外层两个部分和的最大值可在第二隐藏层计算，因此任意有限线性组合均为两层隐藏层ReLU可实现。通过在给定搜索空间中寻找MAX_N的恒等式来获得精确表示。

Result: 成功获得MAX_5、MAX_6、MAX_7和MAX_8的两层隐藏层ReLU精确表示。这些表示与Ruess等人独立获得的N≤10的结果不同，虽共享大部分高层思路，但存在一些细微差异。

Conclusion: 工作为MAX_N的两层隐藏层表示问题提供了新的数据和独立验证，尽管与同期工作有重叠，但独立开发的技术和差异可能对未来研究有参考价值，为进一步探索N更大时能否保持两层表示提供了动力。

Abstract: We study exact representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N(x)=\max{x_1,\ldots,x_N}$ using two-hidden-layer ReLU neural networks. This problem has been studied in recent years in an attempt to characterize the exact number of hidden layers required to represent continuous piecewise linear functions. The best lower bound is 2, while the current upper bound is logarithmic in $N$. It remains completely open if the right answer is a constant number of hidden layers (possibly even 2!) or not. In fact, a recent breakthrough was the representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_5$ as a two-hidden-layer ReLU function obtained in [Bakaev et al., 2026], and the case of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ was stated as open for $N\geq 6$ in that paper.
  Using a careful computer assisted search, we obtain two-hidden-layer ReLU representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$, and $\mathrm{MAX}_8$. We obtain these by considering rational linear combinations of terms of the form $\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$, where $a_r,b_r,c_r,d_r\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$. Each inner maximum of two coordinates can be computed in a first hidden layer, and the outer maximum of the two side-sums can be computed in a second hidden layer. Consequently, every finite linear combination of these terms has a two-hidden-layer ReLU realization. An identity for $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ in this form therefore gives an exact two-hidden-layer ReLU representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$.
  Very recently, two-hidden-layer representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ of the above form were obtained for all $N\leq 10$ in [Ruess et al., 2026]. Our representations are different and were developed independently. While our techniques share most of the high-level ideas presented in [Ruess et al., 2026], there are also some minor differences which may be of interest for future research on this problem.

</details>


### [261] [Trust the Mass: Forced Weights in KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25230)
*Jack Shi,Jerry Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模枚举实验发现，稀疏注意力/KV缓存驱逐中保留最大权重已接近最优（最佳子集仅多弥合2-5%的与全注意力的差距）；进一步测量字节后发现许多号称最强的方法实际存储的是掩码而非稀疏化数据，并未节省内存。FIFO/ContourKV等方法的巨大优势可能源于数据泄漏（在问题可见时计算排序），作者提出ContourKV——基于drop-mass统计的无训练分配器，在160次配对比较中赢得93次，并在字节预算一致时与最强基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力或KV缓存驱逐方法都通过保留部分键、丢弃其余键并重新归一化注意力权重来工作，但已发表的方法之间的性能差距来源不明。作者希望弄清：①最优的键子集选择究竟能带来多少提升；②已发表方法之间的差距是否来自选择规则本身的优劣，还是来自其他因素（如内存占用或实验设置）。

Method: ①在五个模型的168,192个注意力行上枚举最优键子集，与保留最大权重的基线比较；②测量各驱逐方法实际持有的字节数（区分掩码存储与冗余存储）；③在统一评估管道下施加名义预算并测量性能损失；④构造基于"丢弃质量"（dropped-mass）统计的无训练分配器ContourKV，与最先进方法进行成对比较。

Result: ①最优子集相对于保留最大权重仅额外弥合2-5%的与全注意力差距，说明保留最大权重已接近最优选择；②最强查询无关（query-agnostic）方法实际持有完整缓存，因为其按头选择以掩码形式存储，只有不规则的按头存储才能真正释放内存；③在固定选择上强制名义预算导致14-62个基准点的性能损失；④一个87.6分的检索差距可追溯到"问题可见时计算排序"这一泄漏设置；⑤ContourKV在160次配对比较中赢93次、输22次，在字节预算与预算强制基线一致时与最强方法持平。

Conclusion: 已发表驱逐方法之间的性能差距大部分并非来自"选择哪些键"这一决策本身（因为保留最大权重已近乎最优），而是来自字节占用差异、评估设置以及潜在的数据泄漏。任何声称优于保留最大权重基线的方法必须在相同字节预算和公平评估下验证其优势。

Abstract: Every deployed sparse-attention or KV-cache-eviction rule keeps a subset of the keys, discards the rest, and renormalizes the attention weights over the kept set. Enumerating the exact best subset under that constraint on $168{,}192$ attention rows from five models shows that keeping the largest weights is already near-optimal, since the best subset closes only a median $2$ to $5\%$ of the remaining gap to full attention. If selection closes this little, published margins between eviction methods must come from elsewhere, so we measure the bytes each method holds. In the shared evaluation pipeline, the strongest query-agnostic methods hold the full cache because their per-head selections are stored as masks, and only ragged per-head storage frees that memory. Enforcing a nominal budget on one fixed selection costs $14$ to $62$ benchmark points. We trace an $87.6$-point retrieval margin to rankings computed while the question is visible. ContourKV, a training-free allocator built from the dropped-mass statistic, wins $93$ of $160$ paired comparisons against that state of the art and loses $22$ at the byte count of the budget-enforcing baselines, and it ties the strongest of them.

</details>


### [262] [Output Dilution: Redundant but Fragile Representations in MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25231)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE模型（OLMoE-1B-7B）虽能像稠密模型一样稳健地编码道德内容，但其表征在激活噪声下极其脆弱，鲁棒性相差4.2倍；原因在于MoE块在聚合专家输出时产生输出稀释，使进入残差流的信号比稠密MLP小近两个数量级，道德信息虽完整保留却极易被扰动淹没。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注MoE模型在性能上是否接近稠密模型，但鲜有从表征鲁棒性角度审视稀疏架构的脆弱性。作者希望揭示MoE模型在编码稀疏性与表征质量之间可能存在的不匹配。

Method: 在OLMoE-1B-7B中，使用线性探针从各专家-层组合上恢复道德效价（valence）信息，并施加不同程度的激活噪声，与匹配规模稠密模型进行对照实验；同时通过检查点轨迹分析探明脆弱性的来源是架构特性还是训练不足。

Result: 线性探针在几乎所有专家-层组合上以超过90%的平均峰值精度恢复道德效价；但在稠密模型能轻松承受的噪声水平下，MoE表征即崩溃，鲁棒性相差4.2倍。输出稀释是根本原因：MoE块的平均聚合使前馈信号比稠密MLP小近两个数量级。路由本身在噪声下保持稳定，脆弱性完全源于被稀释的聚合输出。检查点轨迹表明专家从未特化，准确率在前几千步内即饱和，确认这是架构性而非习得性问题。

Conclusion: 在稀疏架构中，冗余编码（每个专家-层都含道德信息）并不意味着鲁棒编码。MoE通过平均聚合实现的输出稀释是一个架构层面的弱点，即使信息内容等价于稠密模型，其信号尺度也使其极易被扰动淹没，这为稀疏模型在噪声环境下的可解释性与安全性应用提出了挑战。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models appear to encode moral content as robustly as dense models, yet prove far more fragile in their encoding. In OLMoE-1B-7B, linear probes recover moral valence from nearly every expert-layer combination, with mean peak-layer accuracy above 90%. But these representations collapse under levels of activation noise that a dense model of matched size easily tolerates, with a 4.2-fold difference in robustness.
  We trace this to output dilution. Because the MoE block averages across active experts before contributing to the residual stream, the feedforward signal reaching downstream layers is nearly two orders of magnitude smaller than in a dense MLP. Moral information, our interest, survives aggregation intact but at a scale trivially overwhelmed by perturbation. Routing itself remains stable under noise while the vulnerability originates entirely in the diluted aggregate.
  Checkpoint trajectories confirm this is architectural, not learned. Experts never specialize and accuracy saturates within the first few thousand steps. In sparse architectures, redundant encoding does not imply robust encoding.

</details>


### [263] [Long-Term Behavioral Evaluation for Trusted Collaborator Selection via Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25232)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于双向Mamba的模型(BM)，用于设备长期行为评估。通过将较短时间段内设备间的历史协作构建为图并聚合行为特征，再利用双向Mamba模型整合所有时间段的短期表示，生成设备长期行为评价，从而支持更可靠的协作伙伴选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择可信赖的协作者对协作任务成功至关重要，而当前基于历史协作的设备行为评估面临两大挑战：(1) 基于有限历史协作的行为评估只捕捉瞬时过去行为，无法真实反映设备行为；(2) 设备行为具有时间依赖特性，仅依赖早期协作的单向评估会错过从后续协作中学习的机会。因此需要从长期协作中同时考虑前向和后向时间依赖来评估设备行为。

Method: 提出双向Mamba模型(BM)用于长期行为评估。具体流程为：1) 在每个短时间段内，基于历史协作在设备间构建图，并聚合该时间段内的设备行为特征；2) 使用双向Mamba模型整合所有时间区间的短期表示，产生每个设备稳定且可靠的长期行为评估结果。

Result: 实验结果表明，BM在行为评估准确性上优于基线方法，从而能够选择最大化任务完成价值的协作者。

Conclusion: 通过双向Mamba整合短期行为表征，模型能够充分利用长期协作中前后向的时间依赖信息，获得更稳定可靠的设备长期行为评估，最终提升协作伙伴选择的准确性和任务完成价值。

Abstract: Effective selection of trustworthy collaborators is crucial to ensuring the successful completion of collaborative tasks, which requires accurate assessments of both long-term device behavior and short-term collaborative dynamics. Consistent device behavior patterns, which are learned from historical collaborations, can be used to predict their reliability in future collaborations. However, accurately assessing device behavior based on historical collaborations remains challenging. First, behavior assessment from limited historical collaborations captures only instantaneous past behavior, failing to represent the devices' true behavior. Second, due to the temporal dependencies of device behavior, a unidirectional evaluation that relies only on earlier collaborations loses the opportunity to learn from subsequent collaborations. Addressing these challenges requires evaluating device behavior based on long-term collaborations while considering both forward and backward temporal dependencies. To this end, this work proposes a bidirectional Mamba-enabled model (BM) for long-term behavioral evaluation. For each short time slot, a graph is constructed among devices based on historical collaborations, and device behavioral features within the slot are then aggregated accordingly. Subsequently, a bidirectional Mamba model integrates these short-term representations across all time intervals, producing a stable and reliable long-term behavior evaluation for each device. Experimental results demonstrate that BM achieves higher evaluation accuracy than baseline methods, thereby enabling the selection of collaborators that maximize the value of task completion.

</details>


### [264] [ShuttleArena: Interpretable Self-Play in Physics-Based Badminton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25246)
*Peize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ShuttleArena，一个基于物理的单打羽毛球自对弈环境，通过角色条件化策略（拦截掩码与击球因素分解）耦合飞行轨迹、球员拦截、击球生成和击球后回位，并用PPO自对弈训练得到具有可解释性和竞争力的羽毛球AI策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 羽毛球领域AI面临的核心挑战在于击球选择与回位不可分离：最佳回位依赖于击球引发的对手回应，而击球价值又取决于击球者能否覆盖对手的回球。现有方法未能在物理仿真环境中联合建模这两个相互耦合的决策过程。

Method: 构建ShuttleArena物理仿真自对弈环境，耦合连续球飞行、球员拦截、结构化击球生成与击球后回位；策略采用角色条件化输出——接球回合使用掩码化的拦截选择，击球回合将击球动作分解为球方位角、仰角、球速和回位目标四个因子；采用PPO在分阶段检查点对手池中进行自对弈训练，使用稀疏的终局回合胜负奖励和因子特定回位更新。

Result: 通过冻结检查点对局、受控战术探测、回位消融、定性回放及人类数据合理性检查等评估，显示策略在竞争力上有显著提升，且击球几何和回位行为呈现出可解释的对手条件化变化；回位干预实验表明学习到的回位行为对竞争力至关重要。

Conclusion: 物理仿真驱动的球拍类运动是交互式数字娱乐AI的有用测试平台，因为这类任务要求智能体协调执行动作、场上定位和基于对手的战术价值判断；学习策略能够产生可识别的羽毛球结构特征，同时反映仿真器的抽象特性。

Abstract: Badminton is a compact but challenging domain for game AI: a player must choose a physically feasible shuttle trajectory, anticipate the opponent's interception, and recover to a court position whose value depends on the opponent's next response. The central challenge is that shot selection and recovery are not separable: the best recovery depends on the shot-induced opponent response, while the value of the shot depends on whether the hitter can cover the reply. This paper presents ShuttleArena, a physics-based singles badminton self-play environment that couples continuous shuttle flight, player interception, structured shot generation, and post-shot recovery. The policy uses role-conditioned outputs: a masked interception choice on receiver turns and a factorized hitter action over shot azimuth, shot elevation, shot speed, and recovery target, enabling interpretable tactical probes. Episodes are single rallies rather than full scored games, and training uses Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) self-play against a staged checkpoint opponent pool with sparse terminal rally-outcome rewards and a factor-specific recovery update. Evaluation with frozen checkpoint play, controlled tactical probes, recovery ablations, qualitative rollouts, and a human-data sanity check shows competitive improvement together with interpretable opponent-conditioned changes in shot geometry and recovery behavior. The learned policies produce recognizable badminton-like structure while also reflecting the abstractions of the simulator, and the recovery intervention shows that learned recovery behavior is competitively important. These results suggest that physics-based racket sports are a useful testbed for interactive digital entertainment AI because they require agents to coordinate execution, positioning, and opponent-relative tactical value.

</details>


### [265] [Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25258)
*Hyunsoo Yun,Eun Hak Lee,Jiaru Zhang,Ziran Wang,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural-BSL框架，将可微结构学习与基于随机效用的离散选择模型联合训练，通过学习属性间的依赖结构实现政策干预的传播与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散选择模型和机器学习模型主要基于观测数据估计，将解释性协变量视为平行输入，缺乏内部机制来确定当某一属性被有意改变时相关属性应如何调整，即无法处理属性间的结构性依赖与干预传播。

Method: 提出Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning (Neural-BSL)框架，将可微结构学习与基于随机效用的离散选择估计耦合在单一可微流程中。将观测选择保持在图外作为替代特定效用比较（避免互斥选择结果扭曲恢复的属性结构），联合学习属性结构与随机效用参数。学习到的结构通过结构加权属性交互进入选择模型，并为干预传播提供结构基础。干预评估通过更新干预属性、按拓扑顺序传播模型隐含的下游变化、再重新计算效用和选择概率来实现，同时产出预测的出行方式份额响应和下游出行者/出行属性变化。

Result: 使用首尔陈述偏好数据和伦敦揭示偏好数据评估Neural-BSL。该框架在预测性能上与常规基准相当，同时恢复了行为上连贯的依赖结构。在政策场景中，通过学习结构传播干预改变了跨出行方式的预测再分配，并揭示了支撑这些响应的下游出行者与出行属性调整。

Conclusion: Neural-BSL能够在不牺牲预测精度的前提下学习行为连贯的属性依赖结构，并为离散选择模型提供结构化的干预传播机制，从而揭示政策变化对下游属性和出行方式份额的联动影响。

Abstract: Conventional discrete choice and machine learning models are estimated primarily from observational data and typically treat explanatory covariates as parallel inputs, providing no internal mechanism for determining how related attributes should adjust when one is deliberately changed. This paper proposes Neural-Bayesian Structure Learning (Neural-BSL), a framework coupling differentiable structure learning with random-utility-based discrete choice estimation in a single differentiable procedure. To prevent mutually exclusive choice outcome from distorting the recovered attribute structure, the observed choice is maintained outside the graph as an alternative-specific utility comparison, while the attribute structure and random-utility parameters are learned jointly. The learned structure enters the choice model through structure-weighted attribute interactions and provides the structural basis for propagating interventions through downstream attributes.
