Specifically, we show that the SNR-wise minimum exact maximum-likelihood error probability is $P_e^\ast=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^\ast+o(1))$, while the minimum exact error over packing-optimal codebooks is $P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}+o(1))$, where $B_γ^\ast$ is a common factor. We prove that $K_{M,n}^\ast\leq K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$. Strict inequality, $K_{M,n}^\ast < K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ therefore gives a smaller exact error at all sufficiently high SNRs, i.e., $P_e^\ast<P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}$. We also characterize $K_{M,n}^\ast$ as the limit of the minimum Gaussian soft-packing energy.
  An orthoplex-bound construction explains strict inequality: an SNR-dependent perturbation makes one class of limiting closest pairs slightly closer, without changing the optimal exponent or their unit contributions to the constructed family's leading coefficient, while moving the remaining closest pairs farther apart on a larger but still vanishing scale, causing their contributions to vanish.
  For $2\leq M\leq n+1$, we prove $K_{M,n}^\ast=K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=M(M-1)$. For $M=n+k$, $2\leq k\leq n$, we prove $K_{n+k,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=4n(k-1)$ and $K_{n+k,n}^\ast\leq 4k(k-1)$. In particular, $K_{n+2,n}^\ast=8<4n=K_{n+2,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ for $n\geq3$, so SNR-wise optimization improves the asymptotic error by the factor $n/2$. At $k=n$, both coefficients equal $4n(n-1)$. For $3\leq k\leq n-1$, we conjecture $K_{n+k,n}^\ast=4k(k-1)$.

</details>


### [332] [Exact Common Information and Exact Channel Synthesis for Correlated Gaussian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26012)
*Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Yu和Tan在2020年提出的两个猜想：一是ρ相关高斯源的精确公共信息表达式，二是精确信道合成中共享随机性速率与通信速率的可达区域。证明结合了最优传输、Fathi高斯传输不等式和行列式不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yu和Tan在2020年分别发表的两篇IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory论文中提出了两个猜想，涉及ρ相关高斯源的精确公共信息以及精确信道合成的可达区域。此前这些问题尚未解决，对理解高斯源信息论极限具有重要意义。

Method: 证明方法包括三个关键工具：1) 最坏情况高斯交叉熵的精确最优传输表示；2) Fathi的高斯传输不等式；3) 由条件均值协方差结构导出的行列式不等式。通过这三者的结合，严格证明了猜想中的精确表达式和可达区域。

Result: 主要结果有两点：1) 对于ρ相关高斯源对，其精确公共信息确为(1/2)log((1+ρ)/(1-ρ))+ρ/(1+ρ)；2) 精确信道合成中共享随机性速率和通信速率的可达区域与猜想完全一致。推论包括：对任何ρ>0，精确公共信息严格大于Wyner公共信息；且精确信道合成比全变差版本需要严格更高的速率。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了两个悬而未决的猜想，还揭示了高斯源的精确公共信息与Wyner公共信息之间的严格差异，以及精确信道合成与全变差信道合成之间的速率差距，深化了对相关高斯源信息论极限的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve two conjectures posed by Yu and Tan in 2020 (in two separate papers published in the IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory). Specifically, we establish that: 1) the exact common information for a pair of $ρ$-correlated Gaussian sources is given by the conjectured expression $\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{1+ρ}{1-ρ}+\fracρ{1+ρ}$; and 2) the admissible region for the shared randomness rate and the communication rate in exact channel synthesis is exactly the conjectured one. These results yield two important consequences. First, for any $ρ>0$, the exact common information of a correlated Gaussian pair strictly exceeds Wyner's common information. Second, for $ρ>0$, the exact channel synthesis of such a pair requires strictly higher rates than the total-variation version. The proof combines an exact optimal-transport representation of the worst-case Gaussian cross-entropy, Fathi's Gaussian transport inequality, and a determinant inequality arising from the covariance structure of the conditional means.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [Hamiltonian Two-Way Coupling of Nonlinear Waves and 3D Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25203)
*Sinan Wang,Ruicheng Wang,Taiyuan Zhang,Fan Feng,Jinjin He,Yuchen Sun,Zhiqi Li,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Zakharov哈密顿形式的非线性色散2D波动模型，用于与3D流体求解器的耦合，通过规范一致的耦合方案解决了2D-3D界面处的波浪动力学失配和伪影问题，在保持速度优势的同时显著提升了准确性和耦合保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自由水面模拟中，将局部3D流体求解器与廉价的2D表面模型耦合时，传统2D波动模型（线性或非色散）存在波动力学失配问题，导致在2D-3D界面处产生可见的反射和伪影，需要一种兼具非线性、色散性和高效性的2D波模型来实现高质量的耦合。

Method: 提出基于经典Zakharov公式的非线性色散2D波动模型，利用其哈密顿结构（波面高度η与表面势ψ构成正则对，由波能量控制）实现规范一致的双向耦合方案。2D求解器与3D Navier-Stokes求解器耦合，信息可在2D-3D界面平滑传递。

Result: 2D求解器的平均波高误差较SWE、BEM和Airy基线降低1.7-5倍，运行速度比BEM快1000倍以上；在非线性和耦合保真度上超越SWE和Airy，仅略牺牲速度和稳定性。与3D NS求解器耦合后，在色散匹配和开尔文尾流等测试中抑制了接缝伪影，整体运行速度比纯GPU NB-FLIP模拟快4倍以上。

Conclusion: Zakharov公式的哈密顿结构为实现非线性色散2D波模型提供了理想框架，规范一致的耦合方案有效解决了大规模水模拟中2D-3D界面处的波动力学失配问题，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Simulating large-scale free-surface water by coupling a localized 3D fluid solver to a cheaper 2D surface model has long faced a mismatch in wave dynamics: efficient 2D wave models used in graphics are typically either linear or non-dispersive. These models are fast, simple, and accurate for calm, small-amplitude seas, but coupling them with strongly nonlinear 3D solvers produces visible reflections and artifacts at the 2D--3D interface. We address this problem by introducing a nonlinear and dispersive 2D wave model based on the canonical Zakharov formulation. Its Hamiltonian structure, in which the surface elevation and surface potential form a canonical pair ($η$, $ψ$) governed by the wave energy, enables a canonically consistent two-way coupling scheme, allowing information to pass smoothly across the 2D--3D interface. Our 2D solver reduces mean wave-height error by 1.7--5$\times$ over SWE, BEM, and Airy baselines while running more than $10^3\times$ faster than BEM; it achieves greater nonlinear accuracy and coupling fidelity than SWE and Airy, with minor losses in speed and stability. Coupling it with a 3D Navier--Stokes solver yields a full system that suppresses visible seam artifacts across a range of experiments, including dispersion-matching and Kelvin-wake tests, and runs over 4$\times$ faster than a pure GPU NB-FLIP simulation on the same domain.

</details>


### [334] [GLOSS: Geometric Local Self-Similarity Learning for Faithful Reference-Guided Texture Fill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25461)
*Chenyue Cai,Anita Hu,James Lucas,Szymon Rusinkiewicz,Masha Shugrina*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部几何自相似性的形状特定纹理生成与补全模型，仅需单个3D形状和现有图像模型先验即可训练，通过几何感知参考块将任意新参考纹理逐块修复转移到完整目标物体，实现可交互、可控的纹理创作，并在Blender插件测试中获得专业用户的正面反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件图像生成器虽然能针对3D形状生成纹理，但仍难以在紧密贴合细粒度几何细节和单视角参考的同时生成完整物体纹理，缺乏足够的艺术创作引导空间；而基于大规模3D数据集训练的全物体纹理生成方法又缺乏交互性和可控性，无法让艺术家从多种来源灵活探索多种纹理。

Method: 利用自然和人造形状中普遍存在的几何自相似性及几何-纹理相关性，训练一个形状特定的局部纹理生成与补全模型。该模型仅需现有图像模型先验和单个3D形状即可训练，通过关注一组几何感知参考块进行引导，并通过逐块修复的方式将任意新参考纹理转移到目标物体的完整纹理上。此外还支持局部几何条件的纹理修复，并推广到PBR材质和未见网格。

Result: 与强图像条件纹理生成基线相比，该方法的生成质量达到相当或更优的水平；在Blender插件试点中，多位3D纹理专业人士对其可控性、实用性和创作灵活性给予正面反馈。

Conclusion: 局部纹理化是一个有前景的研究方向，通过几何自相似性和形状特定学习可以实现兼具高质量、可控性和交互性的纹理生成，而无需依赖大规模3D数据集。

Abstract: Using conditional image generators, texture artists can explore many single-view looks for an existing 3D shape. Despite impressive progress, state-of-the-art generative methods still struggle to generate a full object texture while closely adhering to fine scale geometric detail and single view references, leaving little room for artists guidance. Furthermore, current automatic models lack the flexibility for artist to explore multiple textures from varied sources in an interactive and controllable manner. Unlike methods trained on large 3D datasets that generate full object textures from global guidance, our work explores a local and less data-hungry approach to texture with explicit artist control.
  We leverage the geometric self-similarity and geometry-texture correlation existing in many natural and man-made shapes; and train a shape-specific local texture generation and completion model. This model learns from existing image model priors and a single 3D shape, and is guided by attending to a set of geometry-aware reference patches. The trained shape-specific network can transfer any novel reference to the full target object texture through patchwise inpainting. We show improved or comparable quality to strong image-conditioned texture generation baselines, suggesting local texturing as a promising research direction.
  Our model also enables local geometry-conditioned texture inpainting, guided by artist-selected references, and generalizes to PBR materials and unseen meshes for texture transfer. We piloted our novel texture fill capability as a Blender addon with several 3D texturing professionals who reported positive feedback on the model's controllability, practical usefulness, and creative affordances.

</details>
